# S2 汴河 · 运河两岸: the canal wharves: the map in words

Generated by `tools/scroll_map.mjs S2 text` from `map/strokes/S2.json` (stroke points on the scan) and `map/frames/S2.json`. Never edit this file: change the strokes or the frame and regenerate. Scan range u 20500–29050.

## How to read it

- **Station** is metres along the route (`S2.road.main`) from the datum, where the route crosses the seam with S3 (u 20500): S2.road.main's centre-line point 0, exactly S3.road.main's last centre point (20500, 258, est; read from map/strokes/S3.json by merge_fixes.mjs on every run). Station +179.21 there, S3's route station at that point (sections/rainbow_bridge/layout.json), so stations run on.. **+ is east** (picture right); **− is west**. The scroll is read from right to left, so the walk below starts at the east end.
- **Side** is metres north (N, away from the scroll's viewer) or south (S, toward the viewer) of the route's centre line. **H** is height above the ground.
- Directions: E = picture right, N = away from the viewer. "faces W" means the body faces west.
- Positions come from points measured on the strokes. "(est)" marks a point estimated because the strokes hide it (feet behind a rail, a corner behind a tree). Metres also rest on the frame's assumptions below.
- Painted figures are about 1.3× life size against the architecture; positions use foot points, so this doesn't move anyone.

S3's river agents found no vertical face either; river_e's bounds at the far-bank hall on posts (18446-18542: -0.86 to -1.0) fit. 1 m of H moves every boat and shore point 1.5 m north or south (77 / 51.25). ## Landmarks, east to west

- **faint long shed at the top edge east of the 望火楼** `S2.bld.far_shed_top_28720`: stations +321.3 to +314.7; side 14.5 m N to 11.9 m N; footprint 5.6 m (front) × 2.3 m (deep); front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). Faint: a long low building at the very top of the silk east of the 望火楼: a dark roof band, posts and a bench or table line below it, with a small figure (t12's or t11's) in front. Note: Roof band u ~28507-28935, v 37-78; posts at u ~28620, 28700, 28740, 28870, 28950 (v 70-120); benches/tables v 80-120 at u 28685-28890; tree trunks at u 28600-28615 and 28640-28652 (t11's). The restyle invents a hillside with terraced fields, a fenced path and white-walled houses here; the scan has only this faint roof and posts. t12 reads posts under its eave at u ~28700, 28785, 28848, 28945 and records three faint low things under it (crossed sticks 28687-28750, a forked stem 28858-28886, a tub 28905-28940). EAST of it, a curved line (28999,83)->(29079,126), crossing u 29050 at v ~108, with a small upright block at u 28984-28999 (v 80-103): the edge of a bank or an eave, and a post or stone (for S1's structures_w; not recorded here). Back corners at the silk's top edge (placeholder).
- **two long lines at the top east of the 望火楼 (a raised bank or field edge)** `S2.grd.bank_lines_top_28900`: stations +321.3 to +317.4; side 12.0 m N to 10.9 m N. Two long, nearly level lines at the top of the silk east of the 望火楼, about 40 px apart, joined at their west end in a rounded turn; willow crowns cover the ground below them. A raised bank, a field's edge or the edges of a path running east into the countryside. Note: Upper line (28750,150)->(29050,150)->(29200,145); lower line (28780,170)->(28820,188)->(28980,192)->(29073,192); a third stroke runs from (28757,167) down-right to ~(28857,242). They cross the S1 seam at u 29050 (v 150 and ~192; the lines here stop there) and run on to (29200,145) and (29073,192): S1's structures_w records "the bank lines and the diagonal track at the top (≈ 29200-29900, v 90-300)". The restyle turns them into terraced fields with a stone-edged path: invented. Two readings: a raised bank / field edge (grd), or a path's two edges (a road): the lead settles with S1's reading.
- **望楼饭铺: the long thatched eating shed below the 望火楼** `S2.bld.wanglou_shed_28660`: stations +318.3 to +307.3; side 6.4 m N to 2.1 m N; footprint 9.1 m (front) × 4.9 m (deep); front faces S (est: se, ne, nw). A long open eating shed under thatch: a big hipped thatched roof with a small gable vent, a second lower thatched skirt roof along its front, and behind its east half a second big thatched roof; the roofs stand on many plain posts over tables, benches and seated customers (t11's and t12's); at its east end a wall of vertical slats (a bamboo or reed fence) closes the back, a slatted fringe hangs under the front eave and a short valance hangs across the east bay. A long slatted bench stands at its west end (t11's prop). The 望楼饭铺 of the scholars, at the edge of town. Note: Upper hipped thatch: ridge (28353,360)->(28533,353), west hip to (28280,460), east hip to (28700,473); gable vent u 28653-28737, v 400-480. Lower front skirt of thatch v 470-540 from u 28393 to ~28930 (lower edge v ~533-540). Rear thatched roof u ~28600-29050, v 293-480, its east end at ~29030-29050 against the willow. Posts' feet: (28300,575), (28425,615), (28500,660), (28550,665), (28640,670), (28660,670), (28725,630), (28793,660), (28928,610), (28950,605) and the east bay's posts u 29011-29017 and 29043-29053 (v 497-630, t12; feet est). Slat wall u 28920-29030, v 485-570; slatted fringe under the eave u 28880-28910, v 540-566; valance u 28949-29014, v 496-507; the front skirt's east eave end (28926, 524-540). A pale lens-shaped mark on the upper thatch at (28564-28577, 327-350) (t11): a gourd, a hole or a patch. The long slatted bench / board rack (28300,530)->(28420,613) at the west end is a prop (t11). EAST END: its last post stands on the S1 seam (u 29043-29053); the shed is S2's by its footprint (Phase 0). Back corners est. The restyle paints the roofs as thatch too (right). SCALE CUES: eave skirt ~120-130 px above the posts' feet at v 660-670; posts 50-100 px apart.
- **open ground of the east wharf, where the grain porters work** `S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100`: stations +315.6 to +289.0; side 3.7 m N to 11.3 m S. The wide bare bank in front of the sheds at the east end, where porters carry grain sacks up from the boats and the owner sits on the sacks (t10/t11's); it runs from the lower shed's front east past the 望楼饭铺 to the willows, and down to the bank's top over the grain boats. Note: edge_n: the sheds' front posts (the lower shed v ~830-840 at u 27570-27995, the tiled shed v 530-549, the 望楼饭铺 v 575-670). edge_s: copied from river_e's S2.grd.north_bank_26000 bank_top (u 27500-28900; its est points stay est); east of 28900 the bank runs under the massive willow (S1's by base). The porters' feet stand at v ~700-930 (t10, t11).
- **the wharf road along the north bank of the 汴河 (S2's route)** `S2.road.main`: stations +315.3 to +179.1; side 4.5 m N to 7.3 m S. The walk along the canal's north bank from the Rainbow Bridge's north foot to the 望楼饭铺 sheds: a narrow strip between the shop fronts and the moored boats in the west, the trackers' open bank, the strip in front of the gateway and the long house, through the riverside dining terrace, past the open-air eatery and across the wide unloading yard, then along the narrow bank in front of the scaffolded building and the long halls, past the great willow and the jetty, through the grain porters' wharf and along the long shed's front to the countryside. Note: DATUM AND SEAMS: point 0 is S3's route point at exactly u 20500, (20500,258) est, copied from gen/map/S3/parts/structures_w.json with its edges (edge_n 215, edge_s 280). The last point is exactly u 29050: (29050,730), S1's datum: the open ground in front of the 望楼饭铺 shed's east end, just south of the rider's animal (hooves v ~690, t12); bare open ground runs on east at v ~650-860 in front of S1's stalls. EDGES: edge_n is where the open ground stops at building fronts, walls, fences, stalls and tables; edge_s is the river agents' bank_top (the level ground's edge nearest the water: river_w 20500-21850, river_m 23300-26000, river_e 26000-28900), copied at my stations; between 21850 and 23300 river_w2's part had not landed, so edge_s there is my est (t3: shore v ~470-500). No road edge is drawn anywhere. WEST (20500-21750): a strip only 25-65 px of v deep between the shop fronts and fences (v 100-141) and the moored boats (bank_top behind them, v 162-232). TRACKERS' BANK (21750-22300): the centre follows the trackers' towpath diagonal (their feet run (21867,235)->(22172,386)) and passes south of the split willow's feet ((22225,290), (22345,318), t3); river_w puts the bank_top at (21850,248) and the trackers on the top of the wash slope: river_w2's bank line decides edge_s here. HOUSES (22300-22920): between the screen/wall/gate/long house fronts (v 302-414) and the bank. TERRACE (22920-23500): no open ground: the dining terrace fills the bank (its front floor on posts over the slope, stilts to v 485-495). TWO READINGS: (a, taken) through the terrace's floor, from its west end by the long house's east doors and the head of the stone steps to its open east side by the pavilion, est v ~422-425, like S3's route through the tavern's open front (the lead's note for S3); the floor is about ground level at its back and on posts at its front, so the frame may give this range `terrace floor: S2.bld.river_terrace_23250` (a support_ranges entry) or ground; (b) behind the terrace, between it and the hall behind (hidden; the two roofs leave no room on the silk). The builder records the choice. EATERY AND YARD (23500-24600): in front of the panelled screen and the shelter's front posts (v 395-417), then across the wide unloading yard (open ground v ~180-490 between the top shops' tables, the cart and the bank), where the porters walk. WHERE THE HALLS FILL THE BANK (24600-26600): THE ROUTE TAKES THE NARROW BANK IN FRONT OF THEM, not the far street at the top. Evidence: (1) it is continuous with the unloading yard (the porters walk from the yard toward the boats at u 24425-24563, v 400-470, and the strip in front of the scaffolded building's wall foot (v ~500) opens from it, south of the stall under the building's sweeping west eave); (2) people stand on it (t7's two men, feet (25333,568) and (25367,587)) and a mooring post stands on it (t6's at (25207,548)) and the warehouse plinth's capped posts face it (u ~26018 and 26128); (3) at its east end it runs past the great willow onto the wide east bank where the jetty, the sheds' fronts, the sack porters and the 望楼饭铺 shed's customers are, and on to S1's stalls at v ~650-750, the datum S1 needs; (4) the far street (v 15-150, u ~24700-26900) is cut by the silk's top edge, shows only between and behind the halls' roofs, and runs into the top street at 王家纸马铺 and the 望火楼, which does not reach the ground at u 29050 (roofs and willow crowns there). WIDTH (a real-size problem for the frame and the builder): the level strip is only v ~500-520 in front of the scaffolded building (24700-25230; river_m: wash slope v 520-600 below it, where the mooring post stands), v ~555-613 in front of the halls (posts' feet v ~555), v ~600-641 in front of the warehouse and its plain wall: 10-60 px of v, 0.2-1.2 m at 51.25 px/m, plus the wash slope. At u 25450-26600 it is hidden behind the moored boats' mat roofs (est). It stays inside the painted area. The far street is a side street (structures_e). EAST BANK (26600-29050): north of the great willow's foot (t8/t9: (27068-27072, 893-905), on the slope), along the jetty's deck and the lower shed's front (river_e: the jetty's deck edge is the bank_top, at street level, u 27100-27400), across the porters' wharf just north of the sack piles (sacks v ~813-920) and along the long shed's front (posts' feet v ~640-690). Edges east of 24600 are my reading for structures_e to check. All edge and centre points hidden behind boats, buildings or willows are est. MERGE (2026-10-12): edge_s at the stations u 21950, 22100, 22285 and 22450 now lies on river_w2's bank_top (S2.grd.north_bank_22550: 311 est, 380, 441, 430), replacing this agent's estimates (300, 400, 440, 450), as its note asked. From u 22600 to 23300 river_w2's bank_top (418, 418, 407, 420, 432) runs north of this road's centre (410, 445, 448, 422, 425): the level strip in front of the long house is 5-15 px deep in river_w2's reading and the centre here runs on the wash slope, so those edges are unchanged and the frame step or the lead settles it. The steps at the terrace's west end are S2.str.landing_steps_22950 (river_w2's; structures_w's copy retired). FRAME: support_ranges u 22920-23500 on `terrace floor: S2.bld.river_terrace_23250` (structures_w's reading (a): the route runs through the terrace's floor); the frame gives that floor H 0 (its sill v 438-456 is level with the bank top), so the heights do not change; the range records the reading for the builder. FRAME: edge_n set where structures_w had an estimate and the front is read: u 25350: (25350, 470, est) -> (25350, 503), the open shop's floor line v 503-505 (S2.bld.open_shop_25340 sw (25210, 503); structures_e); u 26300: (26300, 610, est) -> (26300, 590), the white-wall house's wall foot (S2.bld.white_wall_house_26330 sw (26233, 592); structures_e); u 27650: (27650, 800, est) -> (27650, 830, est), the lower shed's front posts' feet v 828-840 (S2.bld.lower_shed_27780; structures_e, S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100); u 27850: (27850, 780, est) -> (27850, 830, est), the lower shed's front posts' feet v 828-840 (S2.bld.lower_shed_27780; structures_e, S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100); u 28050: (28050, 690, est) -> (28050, 560, est), the tiled shed's front, v 530-549 at its posts (S2.bld.tiled_shed_27985); the porters' yard is open from there to the bank top (S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100 edge_n 560); u 28250: (28250, 690, est) -> (28250, 560, est), the tiled shed's front, v 530-549 at its posts (S2.bld.tiled_shed_27985); the porters' yard is open from there to the bank top (S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100 edge_n 560); a point (28000, 828, est) added at the lower shed's east end, where the porters' yard opens north to the tiled shed (S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100 edge_n (27950, 828) -> (28000, 560)), so edge_n no longer cuts across the shed. Not changed: u 26100 (the warehouse plinth's foot, v 625-632, is the bank top itself, south of the centre's est v 615: the route runs on the plinth's front or the strip is nil; a question in the frame).
- **望火楼: the brick platform of the fire-watch tower** `S2.str.fire_tower_platform_28450`: stations +313.3 to +311.8; side 9.2 m N to 8.3 m N; footprint 2.0 m (front) × 0.8 m (deep); heights sw_top 1.13 m, se_top 1.13 m; front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). A tall platform of horizontal brick courses carrying the 望火楼's pavilion; a straight wooden stair with stringers, treads and a hand-rail climbs to its top from the lower left (west), arriving at its front-west corner; a railing of balusters runs along the front edge of the top. Its foot is hidden behind the roofs below it. Nobody stands on it or on the stair (余辉: a fire tower with no watchman). Note: FRONT FACE (the strokes): brick courses clear at u 28393-28510, v 203-283, fainter to ~28548; its west edge a vertical at u ~28393 (v 187-283), its east end ~28548 under willow whips. Top edge v ~200-203; the railing on it v 180-197 from u 28400 to 28513. STAIR: two stringers with treads (28290,277)->(28387,187) and (28317,280)->(28393,207), a hand-rail (28280,247)->(28370,163): it climbs eastward against the platform's west side and lands at the front-west corner; its foot is hidden at v >= 280 (stair_foot est). HEIGHT: at least 80-85 px of painted face (v 200 to the hidden foot at v >= 283); no figure stands at its foot to compare (the sheds' customers below are at v 570-670, painted 110-140 px tall). BASE: sw/se are the face's ends at the hidden foot (est); the back corners are placeholders shifted (-45, -40) like the pavilion's back posts (the east tables' up-left recession; with the up-right reading they would be (+45, -40) instead). The restyle draws a wide stepped stone terrace from u 28315 to 28540: the steps are invented, the scan has one brick face and a wooden stair. sections.md's ≈ 28260-28580 is the pavilion's eaves (28313-28577) and the stair. Merge: t11 recorded a long low plank table or bench in the pavilion, S2.prop.table_pavilion_28455 (conf low; plank 28400-28508, v 181-196), retired: the same strokes are this platform's front railing (rail_w (28400, 182) to rail_e (28513, 182), v 180-197), which t11 gave as its other reading. Nobody and nothing stands in the pavilion (余辉: a tower with no watchman).
- **望火楼: the pavilion on the brick platform** `S2.bld.fire_tower_28440`: stations +313.2 to +311.7; side 9.1 m N to 8.3 m N; footprint 2.0 m (front) × 0.7 m (deep); front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). A small open pavilion on top of the brick platform: four posts carrying a pyramidal tiled roof with wide flaring eaves and a round finial on a pedestal at its peak; the platform's railing runs between its front posts. Nobody stands in it. Note: Finial u 28420-28437, v 70-90 (top 70); roof from the finial to the eaves at v 143-152; eave corners (28313,150) and (28577,148). Four post lines: u 28343 (v 150 to ~230), 28387 (153 to ~240), 28490-28500 (153 to ~195), 28537-28545 (150 to ~220). READING (kept): front posts 28387 and 28540, standing on the ends of the brick face (28393 and 28548) with the railing between them, back posts 28343 and 28495 (shift -45 px): the up-left recession of the sheds' tables below (slopes +0.9 to +1.25, see the report). OTHER READING: front posts 28343 and 28495, back posts 28387 and 28540 (up-right recession, as west of u 26500); then the railing would run along the back. The post lines below v ~200 mix with the stair's rails. Feet est on the platform top. SCALE CUES: posts ~50-60 px from the platform top (v ~200) to the eave (v ~150); roof ~80 px from eave to finial; eave span 264 px; post spacing 153 px across the front.
- **tiled roof between the 望火楼 and the 望楼饭铺 (roof only)** `S2.bld.rear_shed_28380`: stations +313.2 to +308.9; side 8.5 m N to 6.2 m N; footprint 4.2 m (front) × 2.6 m (deep) (est: sw, se, ne, nw). A long tiled roof running east behind the thatched 望楼饭铺 shed, at the foot of the 望火楼's platform, hiding the platform's foot and the stair's foot: the second row of the barracks-like sheds, or the tower's lower building; a small dark knob sits on its ridge (a ridge ornament, or a perched bird). Note: Roof u 28220-28540, top edge v ~281-285 (where the platform's brick face disappears), eave v ~345-350; its west end meets the tiled shed's east posts (u 28250-28275). It may be the tiled shed's roof continuing east (then one entity with S2.bld.tiled_shed_27985), but its eave stands ~40 px higher on the silk. Footprint placeholder. Ridge knob (28423-28434, v 279-287) (t11): a ridge ornament (the restyle draws one) or a bird; t11 did not record it as an animal. t11 reads this roof as the tiled shed's, running on from u 28000 to 28480.
- **house with a balustraded loft in its south gable, behind the tiled shed** `S2.bld.gable_loft_house_28060`: stations +311.1 to +304.7; side 11.5 m N to 8.1 m N; footprint 5.6 m (front) × 3.5 m (deep); front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). A building behind the tiled shed whose big gable faces the river: under the gable a balustrade runs across an open loft (an upper floor or attic gallery) on two posts; its roof slopes run down to either side behind the shed's roof and the trees. Note: Gable apex ~(28037,165-170); balustrade v 218-249 from u ~27960 to 28120; posts u 27969 and 28112 (v 249-280). The restyle paints a two-storey gabled house with a balcony. Nobody stands in the loft. Feet and footprint placeholders.
- **long tiled shed with a west gable and tables (the sheds' upper row; 河边店铺)** `S2.bld.tiled_shed_27985`: stations +309.7 to +298.2; side 10.0 m N to 3.4 m N; footprint 11.8 m (front) × 3.7 m (deep); heights sw_eave 0.97 m, se_eave 2.12 m; front faces S (est: ne, nw). A long open shed on posts under a tiled roof, behind (north of) the porters' yard: a gable at its west end over two open bays, where a man stands behind a big tub (t10's figure and prop); further east a long hatched mat curtain hangs from the eave, and two tables with benches stand under the roof (t10's and t11's props). One of the two rows of barracks-like sheds now serving as eateries. Note: West gable apex (27801,205), its west foot (27705,284); posts u 27692 (v 284-438), 27738 (v 297-413) and 27827-27833 (v 300-~425, t10); the tub vendor stands between 27738 and 27833. Roof u 27740-28275, top edge v ~242-261, eave v ~363 (west) to ~386 (east); posts at u ~28000 (v 386-530), 28087, 28144, 28219, 28250 (392-524), 28275 (386-549). Mat curtain u 27930-28000, v 350-500 (hatched; t10). The front foot line runs (27692,438)->(28275,549), slope +0.19. The two door posts' feet (27692,438) and (27738,413) slope -0.54: not a usable receding edge (the gable's plane is unclear). Tables (28032-28125 and 28150-28240, v 454-524) recede up-left at +0.73 to +1.15 (report). TWO READINGS: t10 reads the gabled west part (u 27690-27850) as a separate "upper shed" and the rest as the "middle shed"; t11 reads one roof running on east to u 28480 (my S2.bld.rear_shed_28380). Back corners est. SCALE CUES: eave ~165 px above the post foot at v 549; tables ~90 px long, ~35 px high at foot v 520. Merge: t11 left "a shape between the rear shed's two tables at 28112-28140, v 436-484 (posts or a standing figure)" to t10; on the merge crop gen/map/S2/look/merge_shape_28125.jpg (zoom 5) it is two shed posts (u ~28108-28115 and 28130-28137) and table legs, with no head: not recorded.
- **north bank of the 汴河 at the east wharves** `S2.grd.north_bank_26000`: stations +304.8 to +252.8; side 0.5 m N to 17.6 m S. The river's north bank from the warehouse to the massive willow: a narrow strip in front of the warehouse and its plain wall where the mat-cabin boat lies close in, then a broad wash-drawn slope under the great willow, the jetty and the eating sheds, down to the grain boats, with the porters' mooring stakes on its top and the bank porter climbing it from the second grain boat; no revetment, stair or vertical face. Note: bank_top is where the level ground ends: the warehouse plinth's foot (v 628-632 at u 26000-26230; t7 reads the bank strip at v 555-640) and the plain wall's front (v ~640 at 26450); under the great willow's knob head (est, its lower edge at (26890, 676)); from u 27100 to 27400 the jetty's deck edge (the jetty stands at the top of the slope, its posts' feet on the slope); the lower shed's front and t10's mooring stake on the bank top (27902, 985); t11 reads the top at v 970-985 over u 28100-28400; est under the willows further east. The slope between bank_top and shore is narrow in the west (hidden behind the boat) and 250-300 px of v deep at u 26900-28400 (a long gentle slope, not a face). Bank top taken at street level (H 0). Things on the bank are the tiles' (t8-t12) and structures_e's; the wattle fence under the knob head (26850-26970, v 625-662) is left to structures_e. FRAME: its shore line stands on the support `water` (support_by), like the river's shores; its bank_top on the ground.
- **long tiled building at the top, east of the 纸马铺 (roof only)** `S2.bld.top_long_house_27560`: stations +303.4 to +282.4; side 15.6 m N to 9.2 m N; footprint 12.6 m (front) × 5.0 m (deep) (est: sw, se, ne, nw). A long tiled roof at the top of the silk running from the 纸马铺's east end down to the right toward the sheds, its eave descending east behind the tall pole frame; a second, higher roof band shows behind it. Only roofs show. Note: Eave (27330,93)->(27451,116)->(27551,138)->(27629,149)->(27796,193), slope about +0.21 (down to the east); roof top edges (27323,27) and (27490,32)->(27701,52). TWO READINGS: (1) a long building set at an angle, its front facing the yard south-south-west; (2) the 纸马铺's roof turning its east corner and running on. The restyle paints a long white-walled building with a pilaster at u ~27640 (v 150-300): the scan has blank silk there, which may be its plastered wall or open ground. No facing given. Footprint placeholder.
- **the 汴河 at the grain wharf (汴水悍激)** `S2.water.bian_river_e`: stations +303.0 to +252.8; side 0.5 m N to 17.6 m S. The 汴河 along the east wharves: open water left as plain silk and drawn all over with long, flowing, roughly horizontal current lines, curling crests and eddy spirals (汴水悍激, "the Bian's fierce current"); boats moored along its north bank: the mat-cabin boat west of the great willow, the two grain boats (万石船) and a smaller boat south of the second one. No south bank is painted: the silk's bottom edge cuts the water. At the east end the water runs out under the massive willow at the bottom right. Note: No shore is inked. shore_n is read where the bank's ink-wash smudges end and the thin wavy current lines begin. West of the great willow it is hidden behind the mat-cabin boat (the bank in front of the warehouse shows only to v 640-650, where the boat's roof begins; est v 655-680; the true line could lie anywhere down to about v 850 behind the hull). East of the boat's stern the first current lines show at (26815, 890); the shore then runs down the wash-drawn bank below the willow and the jetty to v 1040-1110 (u 26960-27150; a dark wash tongue reaches v ~1150 at u 27100-27225). OTHER READING at the willow: t8/t9 put the willow's roots (27068, 905) at the water's edge, which would lift the shore there by about 150 px; I read the smudges at v 930-1100 below the roots as bank wash, not water (conf low). Behind the grain boats it is est at the boats' north gunwales (v 1110-1160); at u 28297-28357 it is t11's edge line under the bank porter (v 1203-1234); east of 28400 under willow whips and the massive willow's knobbed head (est). EAST END: the line turns south along the massive willow's west foot and meets the silk's bottom edge at about (28968, 1780, est), just east of the bottom boat's rudder tip (28955, 1768) and west of the willow's roots (u 28942-29255, v 1725-1787): no water is painted east of the willow (bare silk with bank wash at u 29150-29400, v 1450-1780), so the water does not reach the S1 seam at 29050. WEST END: (26000, 665, est), hidden behind the mat-cabin boat; river_m's line continues west. No shore_s: the silk's bottom edge (v ~1780 at u 26200-28700, measured) cuts the water all along the reach. 汴水悍激 (pin 7, u 26670): the current lines fill u 26000-27270 from v ~1000 (below the mat-cabin boat; from v ~890 east of its stern) to the silk's edge, densest and longest at v 1050-1500; eddy spirals at (26300, 1185) (a large one, rings over u 26120-26415, v 1155-1210), (26680, 1222), (27022, 1223) and (26908, 1495); thinner lines continue under and between the grain boats (u 27270-28100, v 1500-1780). The restyle's east half paints u 25500-26400 v 1000-1790 as dry grassy ground (its first draw, kept): it is water on the scan.
- **the lower eating shed with a front gable, by the grain boats** `S2.bld.lower_shed_27780`: stations +301.1 to +289.1; side 4.1 m N to 0.7 m S; footprint 9.1 m (front) × 4.3 m (deep); front faces S (est: sw, ne, nw). An open eating shed east of the big one, lower on the bank: a front gable over its middle, a low front roof and a flat awning on posts, a long counter beam at the back with slatted blinds and two dark cloths hung on the back rail; four tables with benches under it (t10's props) and a serving man deep inside (t10). A cord of small pennants runs from the gable's east side to the top of a tall A-frame of poles (t10's S2.prop.pennant_frame_27930); its capped east front post stands at u ~27990. Note: Gable apex (27697,522), feet ~(27600,600) and (27813,605); low front roof / beam v 600-630 at u 27570-27850 and a flat awning on to ~27980. Back counter beam v 658-662 (u 27560-27950) with slatted blinds v 665-730 and two dark cloths at u 27750-27787 (v 657-690). Posts: west front post u ~27568 (v 650-828); capped east post u 27977-28000, cap v 687-700, foot (27992,840) (t10); middle posts est. TABLES (t10's props) at u 27600-27740 and 27855-27990, v 750-830: their short sides slope +0.87 to +0.96 (up-left), see the report's receding-edge table. t10 calls this "the lower shed"; it may be the east part of S2.bld.eating_shed_west_27300 under one roof. Back corners est. SCALE CUES: back beam ~170 px above the front posts' feet at v 830; tables ~100-130 px long, tops ~25-45 px above their feet.
- **open yard east of the 纸马铺, under the pole frame** `S2.grd.yard_pole_frame_27500`: stations +300.1 to +282.0; side 11.5 m N to 7.8 m N; footprint 10.1 m (front) × 3.0 m (deep) (est: sw, se, ne, nw). It opens south-east down to the wharf yard between the lower shed and the tiled shed. Note: Bounded by the 纸马铺's east end (u ~27290, v 200), the pole frame's feet (v 300-340), the gabled shed's door posts (u 27692, v 413-438) and the eating shed's roof top edge (v ~400-470). A railing with close balusters slanting down to the east between the tall pole frame's east end and the tiled shed's west gable posts, with a lower rail below it on posts: the rail of a stair or ramp, or of a raised gallery joining the frame to the shed. SCALE CUES: railing ~45-50 px tall (rail to rail).
- **the big eating shed east of the great willow (喝杯小酒): tiled roof and mat awning** `S2.bld.eating_shed_west_27300`: stations +290.8 to +276.6; side 7.4 m N to 0.3 m S; footprint 11.3 m (front) × 6.8 m (deep); front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). Note: Tiled roof u 27037-~27600, top edge (27100,430)->(27480,400), eave (27045,546)->(27268,608)->(27480,662), slope +0.25 (down to the east). - **raised plank walkway (jetty) in front of the lower shed** `S2.str.jetty_27260`: stations +286.1 to +278.9; side 0.3 m N to 1.6 m S; footprint 5.3 m (front) × 2.1 m (deep); heights sw_top 0.65 m, se_top 0.91 m; front faces S (est: ne, nw). A raised plank deck on posts running along the top of the bank in front of the lower eating shed, from behind the great willow's trunk (u ~27080) east to u ~27420: its river-facing front edge has a lattice railing or fascia of short vertical slats, and four posts stand on the bank slope below it; the shed's tables stand on or just behind it. Nobody is on it (t9). It does not reach the water: the wash-drawn bank continues 50-200 px below its posts' feet. Note: ON LAND, NOT OVER WATER: the posts' feet are on the bank slope (v 842-947) and the water's edge is lower, at v ~1070-1135 (u 27050-27250). The brief leaves a wholly-on-land jetty to structures_e, but structures_e's brief gives the jetty to river_e and t9 lists it as river_e's, so I record it so it is not missed; if structures_e records it (or reads it as the lower shed's raised floor), the merge keeps one. Strokes: front top edge (27135, 765) -> (27380, 850), slope +0.35; a lower rail about 40 px below it with short vertical slats between (a railing or the deck's fascia); posts (bays) at u 27135, 27225, 27316 and 27385, feet at v 842, 895, 926 and 947 (+-10 px); the east end at about u 27400-27420, the west end behind the willow trunk at about u 27080-27100 (est). The north corners are placeholders (the deck's back edge is under the shed's tables). SCALE CUE: front edge 77-97 px above the posts' feet (765 -> 842 at u 27135; 850 -> 947 at u 27380). Reading of the top line as the deck edge (with the restyle, which draws a plank floor there with tables on it): conf med; the other reading is that the top line is a railing's top rail and the deck lies about 40 px lower. The restyle paves the bank below it with flagstones: not painted. For the tiles' support `jetty deck: S2.str.jetty`, read this ID.
- **the far street behind the halls, at the top of the silk** `S2.road.far_street_26200`: stations +282.4 to +227.0; side 11.8 m N to 4.2 m N. A street at the very top of the silk behind the long riverside halls: sheds with tables, a stall awning, a round-topped cart, animals and a few people along it; east of the great willow at the top it opens into the wide ground in front of the 王家纸马铺. Note: No edge is drawn. edge_n: the sheds' and huts' fronts (S2.bld.far_shed_25785, far_long_shed_26260, mat_hut_26515) and the 纸马铺's floor line (v 200-203). edge_s: hidden behind the 沿河雅座/warehouse roof (top edge v ~145) as far as u ~26450; east of it the open ground under the willow slopes down to the bank, and in front of the 纸马铺 the ground runs to the low shed's roof (v ~323-330). The far street's figures stand at v 130-147 (t7). WEST END: at u 24620-24850 it shows above the scaffold house's banner pole, where t6's man by the pole stack (foot 24732,116) and basket porter (24804,120) walk; it starts at the open yard north-east of the cart (u 24500-24700, v 100-180, structures_w's S2.road.main side), and from u ~24850 to 25500 it is hidden behind the scaffold and the tall rear hall's roof (v 20-200; est). A side street, not the route (S2.road.main is structures_w's): see my report for the reading of the route.
- **王家纸马铺: the Wang family's paper-offerings shop** `S2.bld.wang_paper_horse_shop_27100`: stations +281.9 to +273.1; side 15.4 m N to 9.0 m N; footprint 10.7 m (front) × 3.6 m (deep); heights sw_eave 1.32 m, se_eave 1.3 m; front faces S (est: ne, nw). A long single-storey shop at the top of the wide east bank, open along its whole front under a tiled roof with a heavy eave beam: shelves and counters stacked with paper goods (paper horses and offerings for the Qingming rites). Its tall signboard on a post stands at its west end (t8's prop: 王家紙馬…); a tall stand heaped with paper offerings stands at its front (a prop, t9's). At its east end a light side wall and the roof's end turn back north; at its west end the roof ends in a rounded hip or gable end above the sign, with a dark slanting strut (an eave bracket, or the sign's hook) above the sign's cap. Note: Roof v 20-90, eave v 90-105, eave beam v ~100-105; open front v 105-200 with shelves and goods; floor / step line v 195-203 from u 26915 to 27290. Posts at u ~26920, 26940, 26980, 27050, 27120, 27175, 27275-27287. East end: a light wall u 27287-27340, v 100-196, and the roof's edge rising (27287,103)->(27430,50) (its east verge or hip running back, slope -0.37). The sign (u 26880-26913, v 70-222) stands ~5 px west of the shop's west post. The paper-offering stand u 27045-27120, v 100-242 (base v ~240) stands in front of the posts at 27050-27120. Main point at u ~27100: sections.md's ≈ 26850 is the sign. Back corners at the silk's top edge (placeholder). WEST END: a rounded light shape with a dark outline at u 26905-26940, v 35-110, read as the roof's west hip or gable end (the restyle paints the roof's west end there; t9: or a bare tree top behind the roof, or a lamp); a dark slanted bar (26873,47)->(26910,63) above the sign's cap (t8: an eave strut or a hook on the sign). SCALE CUES: eave ~100 px above the floor line at v 200; front 375 px long.
- **low wattle fence (or the willow's knobbed foot) with a small post-and-rail frame, under the great willow** `S2.str.wattle_fence_26890`: stations +275.8 to +272.8; side 0.9 m N to 0.4 m S; footprint 3.0 m (front) × 0.6 m (deep); heights sw_top 0.66 m; front faces S (est: sw, ne, nw). If (2), the merge keeps only the frame (as a t8/t9 prop). SCALE CUES: ~50 px tall at foot v 676; frame 35 px wide.

Recorded as a building of its own because the front changes (closed lattice doors on a plinth vs. the hall's open raised room); if the builder makes one roof for both, that is the painting. - **north bank of the river, u 23300-26000** `S2.grd.bank_n_23300`: stations +253.0 to +214.3; side on the centre line to 1.6 m S. The north bank: a narrow strip of open ground between the eatery, the street and the halls and the water, drawn as bare silk ending in a band of grey ink wash where it slopes down to the boats. Nowhere a vertical face, stair or quay in this reach; the boats lie with their north sides against the slope. Note: bank_top = where the flat ground ends and the wash slope begins. 23300-23700: the terrace (structures_w) and the eatery's front (bench feet v ~405, t4) stand above it; the dark wash under the terrace runs v ~460-500. 23700-24600: the porters walk at v 330-470 (t5); wash v ~480-530; a ladder from the cargo boat's deck leans up to the bank edge at (24045, 505). 24745-25235: the scaffolded building's wall foot at v ~500 (t6), a flat strip to v ~520, wash 520-600. 25150-25400: t6's mooring post at (25207, 548) stands on the slope; the two men on the bank (t7) at feet v 568 and 587; wash to v ~640. 25400-26000: the halls' posts reach v ~555; the narrow bank strip v ~555-640 (t7), hidden by the moored boat. The bank and the shore lines are estimates; the shore line is the same as shore_n of S2.water.bian_river_m. Merge: seam points set: west bank_top (23300, 452) est and shore (23300, 496) est (the means with river_w2); east bank_top (26000, 632) and shore (26000, 665) est, river_e's (the warehouse plinth's foot; this unit had 615 and 650). FRAME: its shore line stands on the support `water` (support_by), like the river's shores; its bank_top on the ground.
- **the 汴河, middle reach (u 23300-26000)** `S2.water.bian_river_m`: stations +252.8 to +214.3; side on the centre line to 1.2 m S. The river as plain silk with fine ink ripple motifs, filling the silk from the boats along the north bank (their hulls lie against the shore at v ~500-650) down to the silk's bottom edge; no south bank is painted in this reach. Three big boats fill its northern half: 逆水行舟 under tow, the high-sided cargo boat beside it and the boat moored before the halls; south of v ~1230 the water is open and empty to the silk's edge. Note: shore_n is hidden everywhere (est, reading error about +-25 px): 23300-23700 behind 豪华客船 (river_w2) and the terrace stilts, whose feet reach the shore at v ~485-495 (t4); 23700-25150 behind the north side of the high-sided cargo boat (its bow deck at v ~575 and its deckhouse roof at v ~496-530 lie against the bank; the bank's dark wash runs v ~470-530 at 23600-24300 and v ~510-600 at 24700-25150); 25150-26000 below the bank's wash (v ~560-640) and the narrow bank strip in front of the halls (v ~555-640, t7), behind the moored boat's bow bitts, mat canopy and cabin roof (v ~600-680). No shore_s: east of u 22300 nothing is painted south of the water; the silk's bottom edge cuts it at v ~1776 all along 23300-26000. Ripple motifs (short paired curls) are drawn all over the open water, densest at v 1250-1650; nothing in this reach is drawn as a strong current (汴水悍激, pin 7, is further east, river_e). The restyle's east half paints u ~25500-26400, v 1000-1790 as dry grassy ground: it is water (the scan has the ripple lines). End points for the merge: west (23300, 492) joins river_w2, east (26000, 650) joins river_e. Merge: seam points set so the reaches join: west (23300, 496) est (the mean of river_w2's 500 and this unit's 492); east (26000, 665) est, river_e's (this unit had 650).
- **沿河雅座: the long riverside hall with open windows** `S2.bld.yanhe_yazuo_hall_25690`: stations +250.1 to +238.7; side 8.4 m N to 0.3 m N; footprint 5.8 m (front) × 9.5 m (deep); heights sw_eave 2.69 m, se_eave 2.73 m; front faces S (est: sw, ne, nw). For t7's figures at the window: support `upper floor: S2.bld.yanhe_yazuo_hall_25690`, floor line v ~462-468, ground straight below v ~565. Roof: one slope from its top edge v ~145 (where the far street's figures stand behind it) to the eave; the far street hides its ridge. Back corners placed at the roof's top edge (placeholder). - **tiled shed on the far street, with tables** `S2.bld.far_shed_25785`: stations +244.4 to +238.7; side 10.3 m N to 7.8 m N; footprint 2.5 m (front) × 2.7 m (deep); front faces S (est: sw, se, ne, nw). A small open shed on the far street at the very top of the silk: a tiled roof on four or five posts, tables and trestle benches under it (t7's props and people); a mat stall awning on a crossed pole stands just west of it (t7's prop). - **plain-walled house with the pole scaffold on its roof** `S2.bld.scaffold_house_24975`: stations +239.9 to +229.7; side 5.5 m N to on the centre line; footprint 6.1 m (front) × 6.3 m (deep); heights sw_eave 1.58 m, se_eave 1.88 m; front faces S (est: ne, nw). A single-storey tiled house on the bank with a long plain plastered front wall (no door or window painted) under a valance of scalloped hanging cloth and a bracket frieze; its roof has a light triangular gable facing the river in its east half, with a lattice board under the gable, and a small hip-gable end at its west end. A great scaffold of lashed poles stands on the roof ridge running back from the gable: uprights, two or three levels of cross-bars and braces, the pole tips sharpened and splayed like a crown (u 24880-25240, up to the silk's top edge). A long pole sticks out west from the scaffold's foot with a banner of three dark vertical stripes on light cloth with a wavy hem hanging from it. At the west end a big curved mat awning sweeps out and up from the wall over a stall (the stall, its table and people are t6's), held by a post at the wall's west corner. No writing on the house itself. A two-lobed ridge-end ornament (鸱尾) stands on the main ridge's west end (u 24829-24849, v 247-269). Note: Wall: west edge (a post) u 24740-24747, east end post 25203-25217, foot v 500 (west) to ~505 (east), top under the valance v ~404; valance/frieze v 364-404; front eave v 356-364 (25204) to ~378 (west end). A faint vertical at u 24867 (v 420-497) is a wall joint or post. Gable (south-facing) apex (25060,228), eave feet (24975,300)/(25162,330), lattice board under it v 304-314 (u 25000-25120). West end: a small hip-gable u 24775-24840, v 312-380. Two small dark knobs at the ridge's west end, (24838,255) and (24867,245): the left has two lobes like a ridge-end ornament (鸱尾), the right a squared cap over a lighter patch like a man's head; t6 recorded both as heads (head_over_roof_w_24840 (retired at the second pass), S2.fig.head_over_roof_e_24867, conf low). My reading: the left is more likely an ornament, the right undecided; settle at the merge. SCAFFOLD: poles from (24880,245) at its low west end rising east to tips at v 20-40 (u 25000-25240), its east edge u ~25235 (v 30-167); its lower edge runs (24880,245)->(25230,160), slope about -0.25, the crown line (24880,100)->(25224,22) about -0.23: it stands along the roof ridge running back (north) from the gable apex, its east part above the tall rear hall's west roof slope. Second reading: a free-standing building scaffold on the ground behind the house, its feet hidden by the roofs. The restyle draws it the same; the restyle's willow at u 24600-24900 is the scaffold's pole tips plus t6's pole stack and this banner. BANNER: pole (24745,178)->(24913,183), cloth u 24747-24819, v 183-267. AWNING: a sweeping mat awning, upper edge (24566,426)->(24642,336)->(24790,292), dark underside u 24610-24745, v 336-420, post at the wall corner (24742, 392-496); it is fixed to the house, so it is part of it. North corners are placeholders (back wall hidden), set straight back at v ~230-250. SCALE CUES: wall 95-100 px tall at foot v 500 (about 1.4 m at the bank figures' 70 px/m); front 460 px long. Second pass (middle) and FRAME AGENT: the west knob at the ridge's west end is the ridge-end ornament (the two lobes join a neck that runs straight into the ridge line at ridge_end_w; g_ridge.jpg, zoom 6), so t6's head_over_roof_w_24840 (retired at the second pass) is retired; the east knob stays a low head (S2.fig.head_over_roof_e_24867), or a ridge beast.

The river between the north bank (the trackers' bank, the houses and the terrace on stilts) and the south bank's east end: plain silk with long ripple lines and small eddy swirls. 豪华客船 lies along the north shore, the boat with six men at its great stern sweep is in midstream, and two boats and a small boat's bow lie along the south bank's spits at the bottom. No strong current lines here (汴水悍激 is further east). Note: No shoreline is inked. shore_n: where the north bank's dark wash and weed strokes stop and the ripples begin: v 455-520 at u 21950-22150 (a row of small shore ticks at u 22020-22200, v 495-520), bulging south to about v 560-590 at u 22250-22400 (the restyle's grassy bank there agrees), then hidden behind 豪华客船's bow post and cargo; at u 22955 the landing steps (S2.str.landing_steps_22950) reach the water at v ~488, and the terrace's stilts (structures_w) stand in the water to v ~485-500. shore_s: the south bank's mound meets the water at (21980, 1548) where the open boat is tied; south of that boat ripples run down to the flat bank's edge (21890,1673)->(22127,1733); east of the small boat's bow a low spit's upper edge runs (22150,1692) to (22300,1662) and down to the silk's bottom edge at (22680, 1790); east of 22680 the silk's edge cuts the water (nothing south of it is painted). SEAM POINTS: u 21850 shore_n (21850, 425, est), shore_s (21850, 1418, est: behind the mound's crest); u 23300 shore_n (23300, 500, est); shore_s ends at the silk's edge (22680, 1790). Water: open silk with ripples at u 22300-23450, v 920-1480 (nothing floats there). Merge: seam points set so the reaches join: west (21850, 345) est and (21850, 1432) est (river_w's; this unit had 425 and 1418); east (23300, 496) est, the mean of this unit's 500 and river_m's 492 (hidden under the terrace).

FOOTPRINT PLACEHOLDER under its roof (walls not seen). t1 and t2 left it to me.
- **south bank's flat edge by the open boat** `S2.grd.south_bank_flat_21850`: stations +209.4 to +208.0; side 20.4 m S to 25.5 m S. The flat, light top of the south bank between the thatched roof and the water, ending in a straight low edge where the big man in the broad hat sits (t2's), with the water in front of it south of the open boat. Note: Edge points (21550,1637), (21890,1673), (22127,1733) as t2 read them, checked here; ripples run down to the edge at u 22050-22130 (south of the open boat), so the edge is the shore there. West of u ~21990 the flat lies behind the mound and the timber frame and does not touch the water. FRAME: its shore line stands on the support `water` (support_by), like the river's shores; its bank_top on the ground.
- **south-bank mound: a long low earth bank at the water** `S2.grd.mound_south_bank_21420`: stations +209.4 to +204.6; side 17.8 m S to 21.1 m S. A long, low, rounded earth bank on the south side of the river east of the great willow, drawn in grey ink wash with a dark band of low growth along its crest and a small shrub at its west end; water lies north of it, and the thatched and tiled roofs of the south bank run along its southern foot. Its east end slopes down to the water by the open boat at u ~21980. Note: bank_top = the crest's silhouette against the water (zoom 1.5-2 on the scan; t2's reading of the crest agrees within 5 px: (21600, 1402), (21700, 1402), (21800, 1403), (21870, 1440)). The shore is taken at the crest: the water shows right above it. The body of the mound is the wash at v 1430-1530 between the crest and the roofs (the roof's ridge from (21380, 1450) to (21900, 1650) is structures_w's). The dark band of low growth along the crest (u 21630-21810, v 1398-1420) is part of this mound; the shrub at its west end is t2's S2.tree.shrub_mound_21605. East of 21850 (river_w2's look range) the crest falls to the water at about (21960, 1530) beside the open boat. t2 left the mound to structures_w; recorded here as the south bank's own ground feature (the banks are the river agents'). If structures_w also records it, keep one. Height: crest about 30-40 px above where its foot would be, i.e. a low bank (est 0.5-1 m). Merge: river_w2 recorded the same mound as S2.grd.south_bank_mound_21700 (retired as a duplicate); its crest (21413, 1407), (21480, 1430), (21547, 1428), (21600, 1402), (21700, 1402), (21800, 1403), (21840, 1420), (21870, 1440), (21910, 1480), (21960, 1530), (21980, 1545) agrees with this one within 5 px and runs 20 px further east to (21980, 1545), where river_w2's S2.grd.south_bank_flat_21850 begins; the open boat's rope ends on its east face at (21905, 1490). structures_w's crest reading also agrees (it recorded none). FRAME: its shore line stands on the support `water` (support_by), like the river's shores; its bank_top on the ground.
- **big tiled hall on the south bank, seen only as its roof** `S2.bld.south_bank_hall_21050`: stations +208.0 to +201.1; side 24.5 m S to 28.6 m S; footprint 11.4 m (front) × 2.1 m (deep) (est: sw, se, ne, nw). A large building on the south bank nearest the viewer, of which only the roof shows: a broad hip roof of fish-scale tiles seen from above, its ridge running east-south-east from a high point over u 20890, a hip down to the north-west corner, eave brackets under its west end and the tall dark willow (t1's) beside it; people stand and sit on the ground just north of it (t1's group). Note: Roof outline (zoom 1.5, enhanced): north-west hip, a double line with ridge tiles, (20570,1547)->(20887,1323) (slope -0.71, a high line); ridge (20887,1323)->(20993,1377)->(21500,1557) (slope +0.36: the building runs WNW-ESE, askew to the picture, like the bank); east hip (21360,1790)->(21500,1663) going down to the silk's edge; eave brackets under the west end at u 20580-20647, v 1603-1703, and a lower band of straight-hatched roofing at v 1677-1757 there (a lower eave or a second roof). Tiles fill v 1325-1790. Facing: its front is not seen, so no facing_deg is given. FOOTPRINT IS A PLACEHOLDER: the base is below the silk (rule: bases at the silk's bottom edge are estimates); corners clamped to v 1795 at the south. The builder settles it as choice:. t1 left it to me ("apex ~(20880,1325); west hip down to ~(20575,1540); ridge east to ~(21460,1550)"). Main point u ~21057: S2's (west end 20570 is east of the seam). S3's open_shop_20000 ne corner (20575,1400) lies just west of it.
- **the 汴河, west reach of the canal (u 20500-21850)** `S2.water.bian_river_w`: stations +208.0 to +179.2; side 0.6 m N to 19.3 m S. The river east of the Rainbow Bridge: plain silk with ink ripple ticks and round swirl eddies, filling the silk from the north bank's moored boats (v ~200-500) down to the south bank at v ~1240-1440; in the middle two great willows on the south bank throw their whips over the water (u 20500-22000, v 450-1300). Boats moored along the north bank (a long cargo boat, a mat-roofed boat and a big cabin boat), a small covered boat and an open skiff off the south bank, and a man standing in the open water among the willow whips. Note: No shoreline is inked west of u ~21780; the lines are where the land or the boats stop and the ripples begin. SEAM POINTS: u 20500 shore_n (20500, 330, est) and shore_s (20500, 1235, est) are S3's river_e points (S3.water.bian_river_18300), copied; u 21850 shore_n (21850, 345) and shore_s (21850, 1432) for river_w2. shore_n: at 20500-20600 behind the north-bank cargo boat's mat roof, just south of the top willows' feet (bank top v ~272-290); at 20650-21100 it runs under the mooring rope's west end and behind the mat-shelter boat (its far side north of v ~240); at 21100-21700 behind the big cabin boat's far side, just south of the bank strip in front of the shops (figures' feet at v ~125-146, the strip's edge v ~160-175); from ~21780 it is the lower edge of the wash-drawn bank slope below the trackers' path (visible, v 330-345). shore_s: at 20500-20640 behind S3's boat moored along the south bank (S3's est v ~1220-1235); at 20640-20900 behind the branches of the dark-trunked tree (t1's S2.tree.dark_trunk_willow_20530) and the willow whips, north of the big tiled roof (top v ~1310-1340); at 21000-21300 between the covered boat's waterline (v ~1240-1250) and t1's people on the bank (feet v ~1370-1440), est +-40 px; at 21300-21420 at the great willow's foot (S2.tree.willow_great_21345, t2's, base est (21345, 1450)); from 21420 east it is the crest of the south-bank mound (S2.grd.mound_south_bank_21420), whose north foot is hidden right behind its silhouette. CURRENT: ripples are ticks and loops, densest in the open water at u 20500-20950, v 500-620 (round eddies at (20540, 590), (20640, 540), (20900, 560)) and along the north boats' waterline; strong swirls at (21880, 450) and (21250, 1145) and (21540, 1290); no bands of fierce current (汴水悍激 is river_e's, u ~26670). The restyle paints the water the same; it adds nothing on the water here except a tiled roof on the covered boat and a child beside the man in the water. Merge: seam points set so the reaches join: (20500, 310) est is S3's north shore (map/strokes/S3.json; this unit had 330); at u 21850 the north shore is (21850, 345) est on both sides (river_w2 had 425: on the merge crop gen/map/S2/look/merge_seam_21850.jpg the ripple lines start at v ~345-350) and the south shore is the mound's crest (21850, 1432).
- **south bank of the canal, u 20500-21420 (west of the mound)** `S2.grd.south_bank_20500`: stations +206.9 to +180.2; side 17.9 m S to 20.7 m S. The near (south) bank at the west end: low ground under the big tiled roofs at the silk's bottom, crossed at its west end by the branches of the dark-trunked tree, the people standing and sitting at the water's edge opposite the small covered boat, and the great knobbed willow at its east end, whose crown hangs over the river. Note: Never drawn as an edge. bank_top read as where land things begin: S3's south bank point (20500, 1255, est); the dark-trunked tree's forked branches at u 20560-20750, v 1100-1300 (t1's S2.tree.dark_trunk_willow_20530; at zoom 2 they look like posts of a shed, at zoom 3 they are branches); the big tiled roof's top at v ~1310-1340 (structures_w); t1's people on the bank (feet v 1372-1444 at u 21085-21255: S2.grp.south_bank_people_21150); the great willow's foot (t2: S2.tree.willow_great_21345, base est (21345, 1450)). Between u 21000 and 21300 the bank lies about 80-130 px of v south of the covered boat's waterline, so the line is +-40 px. No landing stair, jetty or post is painted on this bank in u 20500-21420. FRAME: its shore line stands on the support `water` (support_by), like the river's shores; its bank_top on the ground.

Note: Posts u 22533-22541 and 22553-22556 (west pair, v 201-312), 22599-22614 (east, v 213-317); door leaves between, knockers at (22568-22582, 242-253); sill v 306-322 (slope ~0). - **tiled hall behind the white compound wall and the gateway, west of the long house (roof only)** `S2.bld.hall_behind_gate_22480`: stations +203.0 to +196.1; side 6.6 m N to 2.8 m N; footprint 4.0 m (front) × 2.9 m (deep); front faces S (est: se, ne, nw). A tiled hall on the north bank behind (north of) the white compound wall and the roofed gateway, west of the long house, seen only as its roof and its slatted west bay: a broad roof of tiles from a west eave corner over the slatted screen (S2.str.slat_screen_22375) running east, where its tile rows run on unbroken into the long house's roof, cut off by the silk's top edge; under the roof's west end the slatted front with its capping rail stands about 115 px of v behind the white wall's foot. The rest of its front is hidden behind the white wall and the gateway; nothing hangs on it and no writing shows. Note: SECOND PASS (west): a painted roof with no footprint (map/README.md rule 6). S2.bld.long_house_22780's note says 'whether the roof over u 22320-22650 (v 20-200, behind the screen and the white wall) is this house's west part or a second building is not settled', and S2.str.slat_screen_22375's reading (b) names 'the big tiled roof spanning u ~22320-22810 at v 20-200 (recorded with the long house)'; but the long house's footprint starts at u 22650 (sw (22650, 406), nw est (22786, 256)), so nothing would be built under the roof's west 330 px. Strokes (crops z_bigroof.jpg, z_roofw.jpg, z_screen.jpg): the roof's west eave corner at (22326, 104) with its west verge rising to (22405, 50) and on to the silk's top edge (v ~18); tile rows fill u 22320-22650, v 20-200 and run on east without a ridge end, gable or break into the long house's roof (one chimney, at u 22697, on the long house's part); the slatted front (slats u 22320-22437, top rail v 122, a mid rail v ~150, foot (22320, 185)-(22435, 190)) stands right under the roof's west eave. The white wall's foot (22437-22532, v 302-312) and the gateway's sill (v 306-322) are 110-125 px of v further south, and the white wall's coping (v 173-197) reaches only to the slatted front's foot level: so the wall and the gate stand in front of this hall with a narrow yard (about 2 m at the depth rate) between them, and the gateway's own small roof sits under this roof's eave. FOOTPRINT: sw is the slatted front's west foot (measured); the front is carried east along the slats' foot line (slope +0.04) to se (22640, 199, est) at the long house's west corner post (u ~22650); back corners est 150 px of v behind along slope -1.1 (structures_w's placeholder rule for this stretch), at the silk's top edge. The footprint touches neither the gateway's (back corners v 297-300) nor the long house's (its west side runs (22650, 406)->(22786, 256)). READINGS: (a, taken) a separate hall behind a compound wall and a gateway, whose west bay front is the slatted screen (S2.str.slat_screen_22375, reading (b) of that entity); (b) the west part of the long house under one continuous roof, set back behind the wall and gate (the long house is then L-shaped: fold this footprint into S2.bld.long_house_22780 and drop this entity). The restyle also draws one continuous roof over a lattice front behind the white wall. Main point u 22320: S2's (west range). FRAME AGENT (second pass accepted): reading (a), conf low. If the lead takes reading (b), the footprint folds into S2.bld.long_house_22780 and this entity is retired.

Note: Slats u 22320-22433, v 119-190; top rail (22353,123)->(22447,167) (slope +0.47, down to the east); foot about v 183-190. TWO READINGS: (a) a free-standing slat fence or screen with a cap (t3's reading, taken); (b) the lattice front of a building under the big roof behind (the restyle's). Under (b) the building's roof is the big tiled roof spanning u ~22320-22810 at v 20-200 (recorded with the long house). Its foot (v ~188) stands ~115 px of v north of the white wall's foot. The hatched mass at u 22395-22437 (v 195-290) below its east end is t3's S2.tree.shrub_or_screen_22416 (a shrub, or a lattice side panel: if a panel, it belongs here). - **north bank of the canal, u 20500-21850** `S2.grd.north_bank_20500`: stations +195.2 to +179.2; side 0.6 m N to 1.0 m S. The north bank: a narrow earth strip in front of the shops and wattle fences along the silk's top edge, with two pollarded willows at its west end; the boats are moored along it the whole way, so its edge shows only east of the big cabin boat's stern (u ~21720-21850), where it falls to the water in a wash-drawn slope under the trackers' path. ## Strip map

Each row is 5 m of the route, east at the top. Each cell lists what stands there (× = how many). Structures appear in every row they cover.

| stations | far N (> 15 m) | N (4–15 m) | road (±4 m) | S (4–15 m) | far S (> 15 m) |

Nearest: small bare tree (or a post) in front of the far roof 0.7 m W, trestle furniture (or a wattle fence) under the far roof at the top 2.6 m W, second far tree with a dark mass at its foot (uncertain) 4 m W. His seat is the bench at u 29008-29052 (top v 600-610); feet est west of it behind the donkey's rump. Painted height 117 px seated (head to est foot). OTHER READING (the setup's brief): a rider astride the donkey. Against it: no robe or leg crosses the donkey's dark flank below its back line, his seat-to-head height (about 75 px to the bench, 98 px to the donkey's back) matches the seated customers (63-80 px) only if he sits on the bench, and the bench stands right under his hips. If he rides, his support becomes animal S2.ani.donkey_dark_28921, pose riding, foot est (28975, 684). Crops: scratchpad S2_t12/c_rider2.jpg, c_bench.jpg, g_shed3.jpg, c_rider_nogrid.jpg.

### Stations +315.0 to +310.0

- **dark donkey at the shed's east end** `S2.ani.donkey_dark_28921`: station +314.1, 0.9 m N; 1.2 m S of 望楼饭铺: the long thatched eating shed below the 望火楼; standing; faces W; 0.66 m long. A dark (black or dark brown) donkey, or a small horse or mule, standing at the east end of the 望楼饭铺 shed facing west, its head held high toward the round-faced man who holds its lead, one foreleg stepping forward; no saddle or load shows (the man in the light robe leans over its back); tail hanging at the east end. (名画记: 城乡交界处 (pin 10)) Nearest: stool under the round-faced man 0.9 m NW, round-faced man holding the donkey's lead 1.2 m W, man in a light robe on a bench, leaning over the donkey 1.3 m NE. [identification med] Note: Strokes: the dark neck rises from the chest at about (28915-28935, 640-650) to the head at about (28927-28954, 576-590), where the lead line from the round-faced man ends; body u 28946-29007, v 624-660 (back about v 622-627); forelegs: one stepping forward, hoof at (28908, 681), a fainter one at about (28935, 670); hind legs at u 28963-28972 (hoof v 679) and u 28985-28994 (hoof v 690); tail at u 28994-29007, v 630-653. SCALE CUE: chest to rump about 90 px, back to ground about 62 px at hoof v 676-690, beside men whose seated heights are 91-132 px: donkey-sized. The species is not certain (no ears read at this size): donkey, or a small dark horse or mule. OTHER READING of the rising dark mass: a person in dark clothes bending over a smaller dark animal (a dog or a pig) whose body is u 28946-29007; the lead line ending on the mass's top and the forward-stepping leg favour one donkey. The setup's brief reads a rider on it: the man in the light robe sits behind it on a bench instead (his robe stops at the donkey's back line and no leg crosses its dark flank; see S2.fig.man_leaning_over_donkey_29000). The restyle turns the donkey into two men (one in blue squatting with a stick, one bending): only the donkey is painted. Crops: scratchpad S2_t12/c_rider2.jpg, c_rider_nogrid.jpg, c_rider_z5.jpg, g_donkey.jpg, inspect_28950_610.jpg.

### Stations +295.0 to +290.0

- **boatman (?) among the coiled cables** `S2.fig.boatman_coils_28543`: station +295.0, 11.3 m S; H 0.01 m (boat deck: S2.veh.boat_grain_28095); 11.1 m S of the lower eating shed with a front gable, by the grain boats; standing; faces W. A tall dark upright with a flat top among the coiled cables on the second grain boat's after deck, read as a man in a dark cap (幞头) standing behind the coils; boat crew. Possibly a bitt (mooring post). Nearest: second grain boat (万石船) under the willows 2.1 m W, light ribbed object under the willow limb 3.8 m E, porter carrying a sack up the bank from the boat 4 m N. [est: foot; identification low; part: behind the coils and willow whips; only the head and cap read] Note: Strokes: a dark upright 11 px wide (28537-28549, v 1281-1316) with a flat cap-like top; coils below it at u 28500-28600, v 1290-1400. The setup agent counts one or two people here on the scan and the restyle paints a standing man in blue with a black cap on this spot (plus about seven invented others). OTHER READING: one of a pair of bitts at the stern with cables wound round them. Deck est v ~1405-1420 (the ladder's top at (28500, 1420)); waterline straight below est v 1470. Second pass: still one man or a bitt (conf low kept). The dark upright is 11-13 px wide (u 28537-28550) with a flat, two-lobed top at v 1280-1290 and a narrower dark stem down to v 1316, dark all the way, with no lighter face below the 'cap'. The heads of the figures painted at this size nearby are 17-33 px wide (the bank porter 25 px, the shed keeper 26 px), which favours a bitt; the restyle paints one man in a black cap with his hand raised to his face exactly here, which favours a man. See the retirement of boatman_coils_28562 (retired at the second pass).
- **smaller boat at the bottom edge, rafted to the second grain boat** `S2.veh.boat_bottom_28395`: station +294.9, 15.4 m S; H -0.7 m (water); 15.9 m S of the lower eating shed with a front gable, by the grain boats; moored; faces W; 4.95 m long. A smaller planked boat lying south of the second grain boat at the silk's bottom edge, bow west under the willow whips: a plain hull with strakes and round bitts or cleats along the gunwale (u 28450-28600), a small upright structure on deck at u 28637-28668, an upswept stern (28738, 1578) over a planked transom gallery (u 28680-28812, v 1656-1725), and a big rudder: a stock with a drum-shaped head and ropes (u 28792-28853, v 1576-1676) and a battened blade reaching down and aft to (28955, 1768). A ladder leans from its deck (28575, 1545) up to the second grain boat's after deck (28500, 1420). No cargo or crew read. Nearest: second grain boat (万石船) under the willows 4.7 m N, boatman (?) among the coiled cables 5.5 m NE, light ribbed object under the willow limb 7.5 m NE. [est: front; identification med; part: willow whips cover the bow and most of the deck; the massive willow's head hangs over the stern] Note: ORIENTATION: the end with the rudder is the stern (rear), the other end the bow (front), per the lead's rule for all river areas. The rudder at the east end (stock u 28815, blade to (28955, 1768)) makes the east end the stern. The setup brief's "man at its east end (28790, v 1590-1680)" is the rudder head and stock with its ropes (t12 agrees; the restyle draws the same mechanism): no figure. The restyle crowds about eight people on and around this boat (u 28450-28870, v 1270-1630): invented. FRONT est: the hull's strakes and its dark lower edge show from u ~28400 east ((28400, 1699) -> (28675, 1724)); west of 28400 only willow whips are drawn, so the bow is at about 28390-28400, hidden. SCALE CUE: front-rear about 415 px; freeboard at u 28575: gunwale v ~1590 -> waterline v ~1715 = 125 px; rudder blade about 160 px long. The ladder (two rails with rungs, about 145 px long) is part of this boat (it may belong to the grain boat: either way river_e's). The rudder blade's tip at u 28955 is 95 px west of the S1 seam.
- **second grain boat (万石船) under the willows** `S2.veh.boat_grain_28095`: station +294.9, 10.8 m S; H -0.7 m (water); 11.2 m S of the lower eating shed with a front gable, by the grain boats; moored; faces W; 7.88 m long. A second laden grain boat moored east of the first, bow to bow-stern with it, bow west: a woven-mat hood with a coiled mat on its top over the bow (u 28015-28290, v 1160-1240), a cross beam and posts below it, the dark raked stem descending to the water; the hull and its mat-covered cargo run east under the willow whips; on the after deck (u 28480-28700, v 1280-1430) a pile of heavy coiled cables and a long bundle with two short dark uprights (bitts, or one man and his raised arm: see the low-confidence figure S2.fig.boatman_coils_28543) and a small windlass frame; the stern is hidden behind the massive willow's knobbed head. A ladder runs from its after deck down to the bottom boat (S2.veh.boat_bottom_28395); its mooring line goes to t11's post with a coiled rope at the bank's foot (S2.prop.mooring_post_rope_28272). (名画记: 万石船 (pin 8)) Nearest: boatman (?) among the coiled cables 2.1 m E, smaller boat at the bottom edge, rafted to the second grain boat 4.7 m S, porter carrying a sack up the bank from the boat 4.8 m NE. [est: rear, stern_top; identification low; part: willow whips cover the hull from u 28150 east; the massive willow's head hides the stern; the bottom boat hides the south waterline] Note: ORIENTATION: the end with the rudder is the stern (rear), the other end the bow (front), per the lead's rule for all river areas. The rudder is not visible. The dark blade at the west end (28030, 1290) -> (28100, 1470) slopes DOWN and EAST (under the hull), as a raked stem does; a rudder hanging from a west stern would slope down and WEST (aft), as the three visible rudders here slope aft. So bow west (conf med; the restyle also paints a stem there). OTHER READING: the dark blade is a rudder and the west end the stern (t12 reads the east end's coils and windlass as the stern, which agrees with mine): then swap front/rear and facing 10. FRONT = the stem's foot at the water (28095, 1468); the stem head (28030, 1245) overhangs it 70 px west. REAR est at the massive willow's head (the after-deck gear ends about u 28680-28714). Facing 190: we see the bow hood's front face, so the bow turns a little toward the viewer. SCALE CUE: about 600 px front-rear along u (the first grain boat: 600); bow hood 275 px wide; freeboard aft about 50 px (after deck v ~1420 -> water est 1470). The bank porter of t11 (S2.fig.bank_porter_28355) climbs the bank above its mat roof (feet est (28355, 1226) on the bank, not on a plank). The ladder t11 records on the bank slope (S2.prop.ladder_28538, (28492, 1155) -> (28585, 1102)) does not reach this boat on the scan; it is t11's.

### Stations +290.0 to +285.0

- **tall frame of poles and ladders in the yard east of 王家纸马铺** `S2.prop.pole_frame_27490`: station +290.0, 11.2 m N; 0.9 m SW of long tiled building at the top, east of the 纸马铺 (roof only). A tall open framework of long poles standing free in the yard east of the paper shop: a big ladder-like A-frame at the west (two railed ladders leaning together, apex about (27415, 105)), several tall poles crossing near the eaves of the building row behind, long beams laid across them at v about 150-230, and a cross-braced A-frame at the east (apex about (27598, 120)); no roof or cover. A timber or drying frame (or a builder's scaffold) whose yard below is empty. Nearest: low stand or bench under the pole frame 0.6 m S, man behind the slanted railing of the pole frame (faint) 4.1 m SE, porter with two baskets on a shoulder pole 4.8 m SE. [est: ne, nw; identification med; part: its tops overlap the roofs at the silk's top; its north legs hidden] Note: Strokes: front leg feet in bays (from zoom 2-3 crops; the poles cross so densely that single legs are hard to follow, about ±10 px); long beams (27300, 145) -> (27560, 180) and (27270, 210) -> (27620, 215); apexes (27415, 105) and (27598, 120). The north corners are placeholders (the north legs are hidden behind the front ones), so the frame's depth is a builder's choice. Kind: recorded as a free-standing prop (rack/frame) because the brief names it but gives it to nobody; if structures_e reads it as a scaffold fixed to the building behind (whose eaves it reaches at v 100-130), it is part of that building and this entity is dropped. The trestle (S2.prop.trestle_board_27310) and the low stand (S2.prop.low_stand_27500) stand at its west end and under it. THE YARD UNDER IT IS EMPTY on the scan (u 27300-27620, v 300-450, checked at zoom 2-3 on plain and contrast-stretched crops): the restyle's crowd of about twelve (eight or nine at v 330-440, two by the frame, one in red, a rider) is invented. - **grain boat (万石船) with the gangplank** `S2.veh.boat_grain_27285`: station +285.3, 10.2 m S; H -0.7 m (water); 8.7 m S of raised plank walkway (jetty) in front of the lower shed; moored; faces W; 8.6 m long. A big, broad, deep-laden grain boat moored with its rounded bow to the north-west against the bank and its stern toward the viewer: at the bow two overlapping woven-mat roofs (u 27280-27750, v 1000-1110) over a small deckhouse with windows and posts (u 27560-27750); a long spar lying fore-and-aft along the deck (u 27490-27810, v 1128-1147: the lowered mast or a windlass beam); planked hatch covers (u 27305-27500, v 1195-1295) and the cargo under nets of crossing ropes amidships; a high transom stern with an arched gallery roof (u 27760-28010, v 1155-1220), a tiller bar (v 1265-1280) and a big rudder hanging from its centre, the blade reaching (28086, 1593). A cleated gangplank runs from its north gunwale near the stern (27795, 1145) up the bank to (28023, 1050) (nobody on it), and two mooring lines run from the gunwale beside it up to t10's rope coil and stake on the bank top (S2.prop.coiled_rope_27873, S2.prop.mooring_stake_27902). Moored stern to bow with the second grain boat (S2.veh.boat_grain_28095). (名画记: 万石船 (pin 8)) Nearest: steersman (?) at the grain boat's rudder head 4.4 m SE, thin upright stake on the bank 10.1 m NE, eatery table under the big parasol 10.5 m N. [est: front; identification med; part: its north side lies against the bank; the stern's lower part is hidden by the rudder] Note: ORIENTATION: the end with the rudder is the stern (rear), the other end the bow (front), per the lead's rule for all river areas. The rudder hangs from the east end's transom (stock u 27880-27900 from v 1217 down to the water at ~1500; blade to (28086, 1593)): stern east, toward the viewer; the rounded west end with the mat roofs is the bow. Facing 160: from rear (27882, 1505) to front (27285, 1330) the axis climbs 175 px of v, so the bow points west-north-west into the bank. FRONT est: the bow is round; the outer hull line runs (27268, 1230) -> (27293, 1330) -> (27357, 1400) -> (27464, 1464) -> (27571, 1493) -> (27793, 1514), and the waterline under the stem is taken at (27285, 1330). SCALE CUE: hull 27267-28010 (743 px over all, front-rear 600 px along u); beam seen at the bow from the north gunwale (v ~1120) to the south waterline (v ~1450) at u 27400; freeboard amidships low (deep-laden): south gunwale v ~1440 -> waterline 1493 at u 27570 = 53 px; transom about 250 px wide (u 27760-28010). Gangplank: two rails about 15 px apart with 7-8 cleats, 245 px long. Ropes: two parallel lines from the coil (27885, 982) down the bank to (27865, 1123) and to the gunwale at about (27845, 1150); a frayed end trails east from the coil to (27940, 1003) (t10's). The restyle draws the same boat (blue mats, a windlass) and no crowd on deck.

### Stations +280.0 to +275.0

- **great old willow on the bank by the jetty (leaning trunk, knobbed head)** `S2.tree.willow_great_27068`: station +279.6, 3.4 m S; 2.3 m W of raised plank walkway (jetty) in front of the lower shed; about 9.22 m tall; crown 10.13 m wide. Huge old willow: a thick, twisted, knotted trunk rises from the foot of the bank at the jetty's west end and leans up and north-west for about 250 px of silk to a massive knobbed, pollarded head lying almost level at u 26800-27050 (v 515-650). From the knobs a fan of long thin whips spreads in every direction: west over the moored boat's bow, up to the street in front of 王家纸马铺 (v about 195) and east over the sheds' roofs. Bare whips drawn as fine lines without leaf masses (early spring); one trunk. Nearest: eatery table (with a bench) at the mat awning's west end 4.4 m NE, eatery table under the mat awning (middle) 5.2 m NE, eatery table under the mat awning's east end 6.2 m E. [identification med; part: the crown overlaps the paper shop's yard, the sheds' roofs and the moored boat's bow; the foot is in the bank's wash] Note: MAIN POINT: base where the roots spread at the bank's foot, u 27045-27100, v 880-930 (root strokes at (27050-27060, 900-920) and (27060-27080, 870-900)); just east of it stand the jetty's west posts (u 27100-27140, v 770-850). Trunk outline: west edge (27000, 690) -> (27030, 760) -> (27050, 800) -> (27055, 880); east edge (27060, 650) -> (27080, 750) -> (27100, 830); girth about 55-60 px at v 720, bark knots at (27040-27060, 720-760). Knob head: dark knotted mass u 26790-27050, v 515-650, joined to the trunk's top at about (27000-27050, 600-650). Under the head's west half a rounded block of fine vertical hatch strokes (u 26840-26937, v 624-676) ends in a curved lower edge at about v 676: read as the head's underside resting on or just above the ground (custom point knob_foot). OTHER READING (that is why conf med): TWO trees: a short, massive pollard whose own trunk foot is that hatched block at about (26890, 676) (t8's range, u < 26900) carrying the knobbed head, plus this leaning trunk as a second willow whose top merges into the same head. Then t8 records the pollard and this entity keeps only the leaning trunk (base unchanged). The hatch could also be a low wattle fence at the tree's foot (structures_e's). The brief's hint 'trunk about 26700-26850, v 550-750' is the knob head: there is no upright trunk at u 26700-26850, where the moored boat's bow fills v 640-830. Whips west of about u 26550 may come from another tree further west; crown_w is the furthest whip that reads as continuous with this crown; top is the highest whip tip (the whips reach v 190-230 between u 26600 and 27050). SEAM WITH t8: the brief gave 'the great willow' to t8 by a guessed trunk position; by base it is t9's. If t8 recorded it too, keep one entity by the base reading. The restyle (east half) draws the same thing: one tree, trunk from the bank's foot, knobbed horizontal head, and paves the bank under it with flagstones (invented). Crops: scratchpad S2_t9/z1_willow.jpg, in_willow.jpg, z3_trunk_foot.jpg, z3_knob.jpg, z35_trunkmid.jpg, e_palisade.jpg, e_crown.jpg. Merge: t8 recorded the same tree as S2.tree.great_willow_27072 (retired as a duplicate; base (27072, 893), conf med, support bank slope): the trunk runs down the bank to roots at the water's edge; its other reading is a tree on the bank top at (26990, 655). The brief's trunk at 26700-26850 is the west end of the 250 px knobbed head. river_e puts the shore about 150 px lower than the roots (so the roots stand on the bank slope, not in the water). S2.str.wattle_fence_26890 (structures_e) is the hatched block under the head that t9 reads as a possible knob foot or second pollard.
