- **big cabin boat with mat roofs moored by the warehouse** `S2.veh.boat_mat_cabin_25980`: station +262.2, 5.9 m S; H -0.7 m (water); 6.6 m S of white-walled house with a hipped roof at the east end of the halls; moored; faces W; 12.87 m long. A big timber boat moored along the north bank west of the great willow, bow west: a deep planked hull with nail rows; a gable-roofed fore cabin (u 26040-26150) and a long flat-roofed cabin with plank walls and doors behind it to u ~26530, a ladder leaning against the cabin side from the deck to the roof (26255, 840)-(26200, 720), a row of round coiled bundles (rolled mats or rope coils; the restyle draws tied netted baskets) along a woven-mat cover behind the roof (v 650-700), and a long pole lying along the roof (u 26330-26565, v ~645: a spare spar or punting pole); aft, a barrel-vaulted woven-mat hood (u 26490-26700, v 610-720) with three big round bundles, an open arched mat hood over the after deck (u 26700-26800) with a man in its mouth, a raised stern gallery with a lattice rail and an overhanging stern (tip (26845, 707)); under the stern a big rudder on a stock with a drum-shaped head, its lattice blade reaching down and aft into the water. A post with a round head stands on deck at (26365, 725). No mooring line or sweep is drawn. Its bow is hidden behind the stern and rudder of river_m's boat moored just west of it (u 25150-26050). Nearest: boatman crouching on the cabin roof 0.4 m SE, man in the mouth of the stern hood 9.4 m E, head at the cabin's east end 10.1 m W. [est: front, bow_top; identification med; part: the bow is hidden behind river_m's boat's stern and rudder (u 25850-26100); the great willow's whips cross the stern hood] Note: ORIENTATION: the end with the rudder is the stern (rear), the other end the bow (front), per the lead's rule for all river areas. The rudder hangs under the east end (stock at u 26616, head (26600-26645, 870-892), blade u 26600-26790, v 920-1048), and the east end carries the raised stern gallery: stern east, bow west, facing 175 (the hull rises slightly toward the bow). t9 calls the east end the "bow": it is the stern. FRONT is est: the hull's strakes run west behind river_m's boat's rudder (u 25990-26100) with no sign of a stem east of u 26060; a foredeck ahead of the fore cabin (front u 26040) puts the stem at about u 25930-26030. REAR = the waterline under the sternpost where the rudder hangs (the hull's bottom line runs (26075, 970), (26262, 989), (26450, 995), (26606, 1001)); the stern overhangs it by 245 px at deck level. SCALE CUE: hull 25980 (est) to 26845 (overhang) = about 865 px; waterline length front-rear 620 px; freeboard at u 26300: deck edge v 835 (below the cabin walls' foot at 822-831) -> waterline v 990 = 155 px; cabin walls about 95 px (735 -> 830); fore cabin door about 70 px; rudder blade about 190 px long. The hull is drawn deep: this boat rides high (no cargo in the hull). Waterline evidence: the dark bottom strake with current lines below it from v ~1000 and the rudder blade entering the water. Possible mooring: the capped posts t7 reads at u 26018 and 26128 (v 565-630) stand at the warehouse plinth's foot; no rope from this boat reaches them (zoom 3), so they look like the plinth's pillar feet.

DEPTH DIRECTION: the bed's lower edge (25535, 100) -> (25598, 72), slope dv/du -0.44 (it runs mostly east with a little depth; not a good receding edge). front/rear = ground under the crates' outer ends (est). Second pass, which reading: the barrow (kept). The two lattice crates are not hung level: the west crate (25530-25568, 75-98) stands lower and nearer, the east crate (25570-25600, 33-75) higher and further, one behind the other along a line that runs up to the east as a barrow's bed recedes here. A load on a shoulder pole would hang at one height on either side of its carrier, and the hooded man (25512-25555, v 28-75) is at the west end behind both crates, not between them. No wheel shows: it would be under the crates.

- **逆水行舟: the big passenger-cargo boat with a mast, towed upstream** `S2.veh.boat_towed_mast_23632`: station +236.1, 11.5 m S; H -0.7 m (water); 11.6 m S of plain-walled house with the pole scaffold on its roof; towed; faces W; 19.77 m long. A long, deep river boat lying in mid-river south of the cargo boat, bow west, being towed upstream: a single mast stepped amidships in a braced tabernacle on the cabin roof at u ~24190, its top at (24183, 302), with a fan of about fifteen stays and lines to both ends of the deck; the tow rope is made fast to the mast at (24188, 375) and runs about 2350 px west over the cargo boat's bow, the bank and the water to the five trackers (t2: S2.grp.boat_towed_mast_23632; the rope's far end is in the lead tracker S2.fig.tracker_lead_21867's hands at (21832, 197)); a short bar is lashed across the rope at (23945, 435-472). A long plank-walled cabin with lattice windows and a door runs the whole length under a low mat roof (cabin walls v ~1030-1140); the bow at the west end has a dark arched mat hood over a low foredeck, a carved stem post and two boat-hooks lying crossed over the stem; the stern at the east end rises over a big rudder whose blade trails at the waterline (24965-25185, v 1160-1220), under a mat awning with a big coil of bamboo rope (24900-25000, v 840-930) and two light boards standing under it, a tall one at u 24732-24789 (v 869-979) and a shorter one just west of the coil at u 24866-24902 (v 920-975); a long dark mat-hatched blade projects east from the stern at deck level to (25285, 1010) (a sweep 橹 shipped aft, or the stern awning's eave). Coiled ropes and bundles on the roof. Crew: a bowman under the hood, a man fending off 豪华客船's stern with a pole, a robed man on the foredeck, three or four men on the roof (one kneeling and hauling a line at the mast), a passenger at a window (perhaps two) and a man at the stern cabin's door. Not moored: it passes the moored cargo boat on its south side, close alongside. (名画记: 逆水行舟 (pin 15)) Nearest: boatman seated among coiled ropes 1.9 m E, possible second man among the coils 2.2 m E, boatman kneeling at the mast, hauling a line 3.1 m W. [est: front, rear, beam_n; part: its north side is hidden behind its own roof; the bow waterline behind the crossed boat-hooks] Note: WHICH HULL CARRIES THE MAST: this (southern) one. The mast is drawn in front of the cargo boat's planking all the way down from v ~460 to its tabernacle on this boat's roof (posts at u ~24185 and ~24222, braces u 24180-24260, v 893-965); a mast stepped in the cargo boat would stop behind that boat's side. The tow rope ends at this mast. BOW: west (the end without the rudder; the lead's rule): the rudder and the projecting blade are at the east end, the hooded foredeck with the bowman at the west, and the trackers haul west. TOW ROPE (custom point rope, a polyline from the mast to the far end, scan px): mast (24188, 375) -> (24100, 400) -> (24016, 430) knot -> (23945, 450) the lashed bar -> (23700, 499) -> its lowest sag v ~518-521 at u 23250-23500 -> (22900, 491) (my reading, matching t3's) -> t3's and t2's readings to the lead tracker's hands at (21832, 197); it runs on through all five trackers' grips (t2). Length along u ~2356 px. FAR END: S2.fig.tracker_lead_21867 (t2), the whole file S2.grp.boat_towed_mast_23632 (t2). WATERLINE: beam_s = the hull's lowest near-side line, v 1200-1215 at u 24300-24900; the bow's waterline est where the stem's lower line meets the hull bottom (23632, ~1100); the stern's est under the sternpost behind the rudder (25050, ~1205); beam_n est (hidden), from the roof's depth (the roof spans v ~820-1000). SCALE CUE: hull 23632-25050 = about 1420 px at the waterline; mast 663 px from its foot to its top; hull side (gunwale wale v ~1140-1150 to bottom v ~1210) about 65 px; cabin wall about 110 px (v 1030-1140); window opening about 95 px tall; the crew on the foredeck about 125-133 px tall at foot v 950-1070. Freeboard for the frame: gunwale (24400, 1145) over waterline (24400, 1212), 67 px; the roof's front edge at v ~1000-1030 over the gunwale; the bow deck (23640, 1068) over its waterline (23640, 1100). Restyle: it adds a second man at the bow (u 23600-23640) where the scan has the carved stem post, and shows the blade at the stern as a blue fish-shaped object. "Rowed by eight men" (sections.md): no oar or sweep is in the water on the scan; the fan of long lines from the mast are stays, and only one man on the roof works a line. Second pass: the light shape under the stern awning at u 24866-24902, v 920-975 is a second, shorter board (retired man_stern_awning_24870 (retired at the second pass)), drawn like the tall one at 24732-24789; a possible second, smaller passenger at the cabin window (S2.fig.second_passenger_window_24050, low); the 'second figure beside the poler' (retired crewman_beside_poler_23748 (retired at the second pass)) is a fender hung from the cargo boat or a bitt post on this foredeck (both readings in the cargo boat's note). Crew 11 -> 10 or 11 with the new low passenger.

- **high-sided cargo boat with a hooded bow, moored along the north bank** `S2.veh.boat_cargo_hooded_bow_23715`: station +234.0, 6.2 m S; H -0.7 m (water); 6.2 m S of plain-walled house with the pole scaffold on its roof; moored; faces W; 18.68 m long. A very long, high-sided boat lying along the north bank, bow west, close beside 逆水行舟 to its south and end to end with 豪华客船 to its west: at the bow (u 23700-23900) a curved arched beam over a dark mat hood, a deck post and a spoked windlass; on deck a flat board platform on legs and big round coils of rope or baskets (23940-24090, v 635-725) and a short ladder leaning from the deck up to the bank (top at (24045, 505)); amidships a flat mat-roofed deckhouse open at the sides (roof u 24275-24583, v 496-530) with a second arched beam and a dark mat hood at its east end (24425-24600, v 586-730); east of it a spoked wheel or windlass on a post (24640-24740, v 523-590); aft a huge rounded mat cover over the cargo, topped with a woven round basket (24775-25137, v 542-667); a high curved stern with a carved post and the rudder stock at u ~25100-25130. A coil or round fender hangs on its south side at (24380, 840), and at the bow a long rope-wrapped fender (or bundle) hangs on a line over its south side, between it and 逆水行舟's foredeck (u 23735-23768, v 745-872). No mast. Its many strakes with rivet rows show it riding high (unladen). Crew: a man at the bow (the spoked windlass beside him is unmanned), one or two men at the aft windlass, two crouching on deck, two in the deckhouse and a man poling at the gunwale. (名画记: 逆水行舟 (pin 15)) Nearest: figure at the deckhouse's east end 1.9 m NE, man leaning forward in the deckhouse 2.2 m N, man seated in the deckhouse 2.3 m NW. [est: front, rear, beam_n, beam_s; identification med; part: its waterline and lower hull are hidden behind 逆水行舟 all along; its north side behind its own deck] Note: ONE HULL OR TWO: one. The rivet rows of its strakes run unbroken from the bow (u ~23710) to the stern (u ~25165) at v ~690-860 (checked at zoom 3 at u 24540-24820, where a second boat's bow would be). THE BRIEF'S "SMALL CANOPIED BOAT" (24250-24500, v 500-600) is read as this boat's midship deckhouse: its two seated men sit at v ~600, on this boat's deck level, and no separate hull, stem or waterline shows under it. Other reading (conf low): a small canopied boat lying in the gap between this boat and the bank, its own hull hidden; it would then need its own entity with the two men as its crew. BOW: west (the rudder stock and the high stern at the east end). WATERLINE: hidden; front and rear est at the feet of the stem and the sternpost where they disappear behind 逆水行舟 (v ~870-880); beam_s est; beam_n est at the deck's north edge (hidden). MOORED: it lies against the bank with its ladder up to it; no mooring rope to a post is visible in u 23700-25150. SCALE CUE: hull about 1430 px (23715-25145); the deck's south gunwale at v ~690-700 and the visible side down to v ~860-880 (about 180 px, much higher than 逆水行舟's 65 px side: riding high, or a higher-sided boat); its crew about 62-67 px tall at foot v ~575-600. Freeboard for the frame: gunwale (24400, 695) over the hidden waterline (24400, ~885 est). The restyle paints knots of 4-5 people at the bow windlass (23750-23980) and at the aft windlass (24600-24740): the scan has 3 and 2-3. Second pass: (1) the 'second figure beside the poler' (retired crewman_beside_poler_23748 (retired at the second pass)) is a long rope-wrapped fender or bundle hung on a line over this boat's south side at the bow: line from a small bar on the planking at (23742-23752, 744-749) down to a scroll-tied top (23731-23766, v 777-797), wrapped body to its tapered foot (23744, 872, est, behind 逆水行舟's bow hood); other reading: a bitt post with scroll ends on 逆水行舟's foredeck. (2) The bow windlass is unmanned on the scan (windlass_man_w_23888 (retired at the second pass) and windlass_man_e_23952 (retired at the second pass) retired as its disc, crank and spoke ends): the crew is 9, not 11. (3) A thin upright stick stands at the deckhouse roof's east end at (24537, 482-500) (t6 left it to river_m): a pole stood upright on the deck, or a stake on the bank behind the roof.

- **man pouring a bucket over the boat's side** `S2.fig.boatman_pouring_bucket_22665`: station +219.9, 23.4 m S; H 0.53 m (boat deck: S2.veh.boat_mat_roof_22290); 8.8 m E of tiled house with an open gable truss on the south bank, at the bottom edge; crouching; faces SW. A boatman with dark hair bent over the near gunwale under the mat roof's east end, tipping a wooden bucket so its water pours in a stream down the hull into the river; his hunched back rounded to the east. Perhaps an offering poured at the boat's side (余辉's 船侧祭神). (余辉: 船侧祭神 (?)) Nearest: mat-roofed boat moored along the south spit 2 m NW, small boat's bow at the silk's edge 6.8 m W, big man in a broad hat at the south bank's edge 7.4 m W. [est: foot; identification med; part: the gunwale and the bucket hide his legs] Note: Head (u 22605-22625, v 1545-1570), bucket (22605-22650, 1590-1628), the stream to v ~1720; the dark hatched rounded mass at u 22640-22700, v 1540-1600 is read as his own hunched back (BOTH READINGS: his back, or a second crouching man; the restyle paints a second man on the gunwale with a basket). Gunwale v ~1595 under him, waterline v ~1690. Whether he empties slops or pours a libation (船侧祭神) is not settled by the strokes.
- **mat-roofed boat moored along the south spit** `S2.veh.boat_mat_roof_22290`: station +219.9, 22.3 m S; H -0.7 m (water); 7.8 m E of tiled house with an open gable truss on the south bank, at the bottom edge; moored; faces W; 8.78 m long. A long boat moored along the low south spit, bow west: a long arched mat roof on posts over its west two thirds (u 22290-22780), with cloths or curtains hanging under its west end; a raked pole (a lowered mast or a stowed quant) leans west from the roof's west post with a cloth (a furled sail?) hanging from it; an open after deck and, at the east end, an overhanging stern frame of lashed beams with a rudder whose stock stands at u ~22885 and whose blade lies along the waterline to u ~23050. A rope runs from its side down to the spit. On the deck under the roof's middle lie a flat woven object (a straw sandal, a folded mat or a basket lid; u 22440-22495, v 1592-1615) and a small staved tub (u 22505-22535, v 1580-1615). A man bends over the near gunwale pouring a bucket into the river. Nearest: man pouring a bucket over the boat's side 2 m SE, small boat's bow at the silk's edge 5.7 m SW, crouching figure at the open boat's east end 6 m W. [est: front, rear; identification med; part: the open boat's east end and the hanging cloth hide its west end] Note: ORIENTATION: stern east (the rudder stock and blade and the overhanging stern frame), bow west under the pole; conf med. The pole: from (22190, 1332) to the roof's west post top (22278, 1505), bound at (22215, 1430), a cloth hanging from it to v ~1560; the brief's "pole or mast at ≈ 22100" for the first bottom boat is this pole. Its rope: from the near side at (22555, 1612) to the spit (S2.grd.south_bank_spit_22400) at about (22490, 1672). SCALE CUE: hull 22290-22880 at the waterline (about 590 px), the stern frame to 23062; near gunwale v ~1585 over the waterline v ~1680 at u 22700 (about 95 px); roof posts at u ~22280 and ~22735, roof top v ~1440. The setup's "two cabin boats at the bottom" are the open boat (21965-22240) and this one (22290-23060). Second pass (west): the two small things on the deck under the roof (crop c_deckobj.jpg, i_matboat.jpg; the restyle paints a straw sandal and a wooden tub) were in no entity; as the boat's gear they go in its `what` (the river rule: cargo in `what`).

His hips are level with the bench top (v ~370), so a seated reading (on the bench's east end, lower legs 26 px) is possible; standing chosen from the 40 px table height next to him. The restyle surrounds him with 3-4 more people (one in red, one in blue) at u 23660-23720: the scan has only him and the tables' edges and posts. Second pass: pose standing -> seated (conf med). He is 72 px from head top (v 326) to feet (v 398), with the torso about 45 px down to the bench top (v ~368-376) and only about 23 px of lower leg below it, the proportions of a seated man. Standing men nearby are far taller at similar or higher foot v: S2.fig.hat_walker_24004 109 px at v 373, S2.fig.eatery_man_standing_23918 92 px at v 301; a standing man at v 398 would be about 100-110 px, and 72 px is about 0.7 of that, as for the seated shop men (59-65 px at v 180-192). Foot unchanged (his feet at u 23634-23648, v 395-399). Support stays ground (the bench is a prop, named here). S2.fig.terrace_diner_w_23083's note uses him as a standing 72 px reference for the diners' seated height (57 px); seated, he suggests the diners' feet may sit 10-15 px lower than their est, within the est.

A very long passenger boat moored along the north bank below the terrace, bow west: at the bow a curved post rising from the stem to a netted knob (a lantern cage or fender basket) and a beam with lashings projecting west at deck level; an open fore canopy with a dark mat roof on four posts (u 22465-22560), then a cabin with mat-curtained walls (22580-22700), a long cabin with lattice windows, panelled sills and a door (22700-23300) with a chair or bench back (u 22960-23020, v 720-770) and a table with things on it (u 23090-23140, v 730-770) seen through its openings, an open bay with two passengers (23300-23440), and an after cabin with hanging mat blinds under a dark mat roof (23440-23700) carrying on top a deck with a windlass (23360-23430) and a small arched mat shelter (23490-23640); a narrow side walkway runs outside the cabins. A mooring rope runs from S2.prop.mooring_post_23126 to its roof rail. No mast is up; no sweep or rudder shows (the stern is hidden). (名画记: 豪华客船 (pin 17); 余辉: 客船启程 (?)) Other reading: bow east (then swap front/rear, facing 0). The setup's brief calls the windlass deck a "foredeck"; it is the after-cabin roof. 客船启程 (余辉) may be this boat: a man climbs the ladder toward the roof where the mooring rope is made fast, as if casting off; the strokes don't settle it. SCALE CUE: waterline 22545-23725 (about 1180 px); side walkway v ~840 over the waterline v ~910 at u 23200 (70 px); cabin from walkway (v 840) to eave (v ~700) about 140 px; lattice window bays 45-55 px wide; the standing boatman on the walkway is 145 px tall. WATERLINE: the hull's lowest strake: (22545, 775) at the stem, (22700, 850), (22800, 877), (22900, 893), (23000, 903), (23200, 910), (23400, 910), (23560, 900), then hidden; rear est at the stern (u ~23725, behind 逆水行舟's bows) on the level of the amidships waterline. The hull lies at a slant, its bow further north (facing about 172). 逆水行舟's tow rope (river_m's) passes over its roof cargo at v ~490-515.

A boat in midstream heading west (upstream), its hull running west under the great willow's whips: a planked after deck where six men crowd along the loom of a great sweep (大橹); a stern frame of crossbeams lashed together overhangs the stern eastward to about u 22420, the loom crossing it at a pivot; the sweep's long shaft runs on east-south-east into the water and ends in a broad flat blade with an upturned tip at about u 22945. The stern's side carries a decorated oval panel with two eye-like marks. Nearest: sweep hand leaning back, face up 0.2 m N, sweep hand with head thrown back 0.3 m NE, tall sweep hand standing upright 0.7 m E. [est: front, rear, beam_s; identification med; part: west of u ~22050 the hull is lost under the great willow's whips (t2's S2.tree.willow_great_21345); its bow is not painted] Note: THE "SKIFF" IS THE SWEEP'S BLADE: the long shaft from the men's hands runs unbroken past the stern frame to (22818, 1089), where its two edges spread into a flat blade (u 22780-22945, v 1057-1112) with an upturned tip; there is no gunwale, thwart or second hull line, so it is not a separate boat (S3's river_w boat at 15595 has the same long sweep with five men and a blade in the water). ORIENTATION: stern east (the sweep is worked over it, the lead's rule), bow west (hidden); conf med. Other reading: the eye-marked oval panel could be a bow decoration (then the boat heads east and the sweep is a bow sweep); not taken. The men lean on the loom at about knee height (a bound loom shows at u 22250-22330, v 940-965) where it runs east to the pivot on the stern frame. SCALE CUE: deck edge v ~975-1000; the hull's side drops to the waterline est v ~1080-1115 at u 22100-22200 (the stern rises); the men are 140-157 px tall; sweep about 700 px from the men's hands to the tip. front est at the last hull planks before the whips (u ~22030-22050) less a little; its true bow is unknown (builder choice).

- **the great pollarded willow on the south bank** `S2.tree.willow_great_21345`: station +206.3, 20.7 m S; 2.2 m NW of long thatched (mat-roofed) building on the south bank; about 14.78 m tall; crown 14.42 m wide. Huge old pollarded willow on the south bank: a massive, knobbed, hollow-looking trunk about 150-200 px wide rising from just above the south-bank roofs and leaning north; at about v 960-1000 it forks into a tall upright stem that carries the crown's top and a heavy eastern limb; long thin whips without leaf masses (early spring) droop from both, the eastern ones sweeping far out over the water to about u 22090. Single trunk. Nearest: smaller figure (child or youth?) facing the tall man 1.7 m NW, tall man standing on the south bank 2 m W, small dark bird (?) on a willow twig over the water 2.6 m W. [est: base; part: the trunk's foot meets the thatched south-bank roof's ridge (v ~1450-1470); the crown's west edge mixes with the t1 trees' whips] Note: Trunk: foot u ~21290-21400 at v 1440-1452 (just above the thatched roof, structures_w's), dark knobbed mass u 21275-21430 at v 1220-1400, up to the fork at about (21310-21490, 960-1000). Crown extents u ~20980-22090, v 312-1180; crown_w is uncertain (whips at u 20950-21100, v 600-960 may be the t1 trees'). Base est because the foot is hidden where it meets the roof's ridge. Main point u 21345 is 45 px inside t2's range (t1's ends at 21300). The restyle shows figures climbing in the trunk's knobs at u ~21490-21545, v 1160-1245: only knobs are painted. The small dark mark at (21357, 1364) on the trunk is a knob, not a bird. Crops: S2_t2/willow_whole.jpg, insp_willow.jpg, insp_wfoot.jpg, crown_top.jpg.

The horizontal double line at u 21095-21165, v 1283-1300 is the pole or board (the restyle paints a tray of wares between him and the tall man): held, so part of the figures, not a prop. No body, hands or feet of a second person are visible on the scan; if she stood she would show beside him, so foot est as kneeling behind him. Keep only if the second pass reads a face in the loop.

- **figure between the dark tree and the south-bank hall's west eave (uncertain)** `S2.fig.figure_under_eave_20625`: station +201.1, 28.2 m S; inside big tiled hall on the south bank, seen only as its roof; standing; faces E. A pale head in profile facing east, and perhaps a body below it, in the gap between the great dark-trunked tree's east side and the south-bank hall's west eave, at the silk's bottom edge. Nearest: great dark-trunked tree on the south bank at the bottom edge 1.4 m W, second bare tree on the south bank, behind the hall's west roof (uncertain) 6.6 m N, small dark bird (?) on a willow twig over the water 7.5 m NE. [est: foot; identification low; part: below the head crossed by the eave's brackets and the roof's hatching; foot at or below the silk's bottom edge] Note: Added at the merge: S3's t9 (head ~(20625, 1645), body to v ~1720) and S3's structures_e ("a figure stands at (20599-20656, 1628-1690)") read a figure here; S2's t1 named the same pale patch (u 20600-20645, v 1630-1700) and did not record it, because at 45 x 60 px it is twice a figure's head at this v ("the gap between trunk and eave brackets"). Merge crop gen/map/S2/look/merge_figure_20625.jpg (zoom 3): a light patch with a profile-like contour (brow, nose, chin facing east) at u 20600-20650, v 1630-1700 and diagonal strokes below it to v ~1760. READINGS: a person standing under the eave (taken, conf low; foot est at the silk's edge, painted ~155 px, as the south-bank figures, 145-196 px), or the pale gap between the trunk and the eave brackets (t1); then retire it. For the second pass. Second pass (west): keep, conf low. t1's size objection does not hold near the silk's bottom edge: the pale patch's face-like part (brow line v ~1653 to chin v ~1700, u 20600-20632) is 45-50 px tall, and the nearest sure face at a similar v, the big-hat man's profile (S2.fig.big_hat_man_south_bank_22045, v 1613-1653 at foot v 1712), is 40 px; a figure standing at the silk's edge is painted larger still. Below the face only the eave's straight hatching (v 1677-1757) and a curved stroke like a hand or sleeve at (20630-20650, 1710-1745) show, so the body would be hidden behind the lower roof band. Which way it looks is not settled: the bumpy west contour (u 20600-20610, bumps at v ~1663-1690) reads as brow, nose and chin looking west (toward the trunk, facing 180), while the dark angled stroke at its east edge (20630-20647, 1657-1677) reads as a profile looking east (facing 0, as recorded). The other reading stays the eave's bracket set: a lobed bracket arm (栱) seen from the side under the hall's west eave (structures_w's brackets at 20580-20647, v 1603-1703) gives the same bumpy edge under a beam (the 'brow line'). Huge old pollarded willow whose gnarled, knobbed trunk is split into two legs like an inverted V: a western leg (foot about (22225, 290)) and a thicker eastern leg (foot about (22345, 318)) that twist up into one knobbed head at about (22180-22330, 50-150); from the head long thin whips spray in every direction, west over the trackers to about u 21870 and east over the bamboo fence and the house roof to about u 22520, cut off by the silk's top edge. OTHER READING: two willows standing about 130 px apart whose heads merge; then split this into two trees with bases at foot_w and foot_e. base = midway between the two legs' feet (est); foot_w and foot_e are custom points for the builder. Strokes: western leg u 22190-22245 from v 150 down to v 285-295; eastern leg u 22295-22380 from v 180 down to v 310-325; knob head u 22180-22330, v 50-150; whips reach the silk's top edge (v about 18-22) from u 22100 to 22420. Trunk girth at the foot about 50 px (west leg) and 40-75 px (east leg). The restyle draws two trunks with a limb bridging them, and two invented walkers below (22200 and 22380, v 290-420). - **big cabin boat moored along the north bank (bow west, rudder east)** `S2.veh.boat_big_cabin_21120`: station +189.6, 2.9 m S; H -0.7 m (water); 3.2 m SE of long tiled shop row on the north bank; moored; faces W; 6.78 m long. A big river boat moored along the north bank in front of the shops, bow to the west-north-west: a curved stem head with a round windlass hub at the bow (u 21110-21190, v 155-210) and a long oar or pole slanting down from the bow into the water (to about (21265, 318)); a flat mat awning on posts (u 21220-21350, v 160-200) over a row of six coiled ropes hanging along the far gunwale (u 21225-21350, v 195-225); a short upright post on deck (u ~21362, v 150-200); a low cabin with a window (u 21290-21400, v 240-300) where a man sits; a big round-arched cabin frame (u 21400-21500, v 200-300) and a deckhouse with a sloping mat roof (u 21430-21660, v 160-280); a high stern with a gabled rudder house (u 21650-21800, v 170-260) and a big rudder hung over the stern, its blade in the water (u 21650-21780, v 345-420). The hull is planked with rows of nails and bellies down to v ~388 under the deckhouse. No mast or sail. Three crew are painted. Nearest: man looking out of the low cabin's window 0.6 m W, boatman standing on the far-side deck between the cabins 1.7 m N, man in a long robe on the north-bank strip 2.8 m N. [est: front, rear; part: willow whips of the south willow's crown cross the hull's lower half west of u 21550; the bow's waterline is hidden] Note: WHICH END IS THE BOW (conf high): the rudder hangs over the east end (stock from (21672, 280), blade (21650-21780, 345-420) in the water) under a gabled rudder house: the east end is the stern, by the lead's rule; the west end carries the curved stem head and a windlass hub. WATERLINE: the hull's lowest near-side planks, (21400, ~345) -> (21540, 388) -> the stern (21665, 387); toward the bow it is hidden behind willow whips and est along the same line (slope ~0.2) to (21120, 300). The diagonal from (21110, 230) to (21265, 318), with a dark blade-like wedge at u 21200-21260, v 290-320, is read as a long oar or pole from the bow into the water (part of the boat); it could also be the bow's forefoot. HEADING: rear -> front rises ~87 px of v over 545 px of u: about 12 deg north of west (facing ~168) at dx ~40 px/m. SCALE CUE: waterline length ~545 px (21120-21665); stem head v ~170 over the bow's est waterline ~300 (130 px); the stern's top v ~255 over its waterline 387; crew painted ~80-85 px tall (est feet). The upright post (21362, 150-200) is part of the boat (a bitt or short pole on deck); t2 recorded it as a bank mooring post (S2.prop.post_north_bank_21364, conf low): keep one at the merge. The setup's 'big cabin boat ≈ 20820-21800' includes the mat-shelter boat (S2.veh.boat_mat_shelter_20805), read here as a separate boat. The restyle's 'railing with a standing man' on this boat (u ~21000-21360, v 150-220) is the row of coiled ropes and the deck post; one possible figure under the awning's west end is recorded with conf low. Merge: t2's S2.prop.post_north_bank_21364 (a faint upright u 21362-21366 from the shop's sill v ~140 down to v ~188, read as a mooring post on the bank, conf low) was retired as this boat's deck post (pts.deck_post): it stands among the deck's gear (u 21353-21385, v 167-195), and the bank strip there is the shop's sill. Second pass: the possible figure under the awning's west end (figure_under_awning_21245 (retired at the second pass)) is a white silk fleck at the awning's tip, retired; three crew.

Hull axis slope dv/du ~ +0.04 (a depth cue only). The mooring rope is a double line (a thick rope) from the willow's foot down to the coil on deck; its far end is t1's tree S2.tree.willow_top_20668. MOORED TO: the willow's foot by that rope. The restyle draws the east end with an upswept prow and the water jar; it agrees on the cargo baskets and the mat roofs.

## Groups

- **the open-air eatery under its shelter (west of the shop fronts)** `S2.grp.open_air_eatery_23760` (stations +221.1 to +216.2): The open-air eatery on the north bank between the terrace's pavilion and the shop fronts: tables and benches under and in front of a mat shelter on posts, nearly all empty; one man sits at its west end and one stands at the shelter's east post. ## Open questions

- **Section:** FOR THE LEAD FIRST, THE RECEDING DIRECTION TURNS OVER IN S2's EAST HALF. West of u ~26200 every ground-level edge recedes up-right (west 43.1, middle 48.3, middle-east 46.6 px/m, continuing S3's 30.4 at the seam); from u 27700 to 28650 all ten furniture edges read by three agents recede UP-LEFT (slopes +0.72 to +1.25, mean +0.99, dx -51.6), the 望火楼 pavilion's back posts stand 45 px left of its front posts, and 王家纸马铺 between the groups (26915-27290) is drawn frontal. The frame uses the measured knots (option A): dx falls from +46.6 at u 25924 to -51.6 at 28121 (-4.47 px/m per 100 px, three times S4b's suggested limit of 1.5) and stays -51.6 to the S1 seam. NOTHING FOLDS, but over u 25924-28121 the rows north of the river STRETCH east-west: x1.45 at mid-water (v 1000), x1.75 on the route and the east wharf (v 700), x2.1 on the north-bank strip (v 300), x2.35 at the silk's top; the route from u 25900 to 28250 is 53.9 m long. East of 28121 the furniture maps square (望楼饭铺's table 82 deg, the pavilion's post line 99 deg); inside the turn it maps 124-131 deg (the drift itself skews short edges). The stretch falls where the painter shrinks the far rows to about 0.5 of life, so real-size buildings there fit their painted places better (王家纸马铺's front comes out 10.7 m, a 3-4 bay shop; held at +46.6 it is 4.9 m). OPTION B, hold +46.6 east of 25924 (one edit: drop the last knot): no stretch (route 34.4 m), but the whole east wharf's furniture, sheds and the 望火楼 map skewed 49-55 deg (139-145 deg instead of 90), and S1 starts from +46.6. OPTION C, a ramp held to 1.5 px/m per 100 px (to +13.7 at 28121): x1.25 at the route and x1.45 at the silk's top, route 40.7 m, but the east edges still map 125-140 deg. Computed in scratchpad S2_frame/knot_options.mjs. S1's structures_w found no ground-level receding edge in 29050-33000 and suggests holding S2's seam knot; its farmhouse hip reads +43 (a hip, low) and its east +24 (low), so a ramp back toward + inside S1 should stay slow (from -51.6 to +24 over 7000 px is +1.1 per 100 px: no fold). Changing the knots is one edit of dx_knots in map/frames/S2.json (scratchpad S2_frame/mkframe.mjs), then `node tools/scroll_map.mjs S2 all` and `node tools/map_layout.mjs S2`; the seam knot S1 seeds from changes with it.
- **Section:** THE STRIPS AND THE DIMINUTION (the lead's note: report each strip's depth; the one-scale frame is kept). The route's strip depth (edge_n to edge_s, m, at the frame's one depth rate; worldcheck.mjs): the north-bank strip in front of the shops and fences, u 20500-21800: 0.56-1.56 m; the trackers' towpath 21950-22600: 2.2-3.1; in front of the long house 22780-22900: 1.1-1.2; through the terrace 23050-23450: 1.0-1.3; the eatery 23560-23950: 1.2-2.3; the unloading yard 24150-24550: 4.2-5.9; IN FRONT OF THE SCAFFOLD HOUSE 24700-25100: 0.20-0.29 m (the level strip is 10-20 px of v); the open shop and 沿河雅座 25230-25900: 0.7-1.6; the warehouse, the white-wall house and the great willow 26100-27000: 0.5-0.8; the jetty and the lower shed 27135-27850: 0.9-2.5; the porters' wharf and 望楼饭铺 28050-29050: 6.0-8.2. THE DIMINUTION (structures_w's table, about 30 standing adults from the parts): against life at the frame's 77 px/m (127 px for 1.65 m) the painted scale is about 0.5 at foot v 116-150 (the far street and the top strip), 0.68 at v 170-260, 0.74 at v 280-400 (trackers, eatery, yard), 0.86 at v 440-600 (the narrow bank), 1.05 at v 620-800 (the porters' wharf), 1.12 at v ~905, and 1.14-1.54 on the south bank near the silk's bottom edge; S2 is painted 1.5-3 times larger than S4 at the same v. Buildings on the north-bank strip built at real size are 1.2-2 times their painted frontage (rule 5); room to walk on the strips under 1 m is a build-time choice (build_choices.json).
- **Section:** THE BANKS AGAINST THE WATER LEVEL (S3's and S4b's lesson: a north bank's waterline must be painted at least 0.7 x 77 = 54 px below its top, or its shore maps north of the bank top). Where the shore is an estimate hidden behind moored boats it is 10-50 px below the top, so with H -0.7 the shore lands NORTH of its own bank top: S2.grd.north_bank_20500 at u 20500-21720 by 0.24-0.86 m (behind the north-bank cargo boat and the big cabin boat), S2.grd.bank_n_23300 at u 23300-26000 by 0.07-0.46 m (behind the cargo boat and the moored boat), S2.grd.north_bank_26000 at u 26000-26650 by 0.41-0.76 m (behind the moored boat and the mat-cabin boat), S2.grd.north_bank_22550 at u 23300 by 0.19 m. Every one of these shore points is est; the south banks are fine. The builder should put the bank's face at the bank top there and the water under the moored hulls (the hulls hide the shore as painted). At the S3 seam the route ends 0.86 m from the water's edge as S3 found; edge_s (v 280) is 0.5 m from the shore.
- **Section:** THE ROUTE (structures_w's S2.road.main, 50 centre points, 20500-29050; it takes the narrow bank in front of the halls, not the far street, structures_w and structures_e agree). Still open: (1) u 22600-23300, in front of the long house, river_w2's bank top (v 407-432) runs north of the route's centre (v 410-448): the level strip there is 5-15 px of v (0.1-0.3 m), and the centre runs on the wash slope; (2) u 25905-26240, the warehouse's plank-faced plinth meets the bank top at its foot (v 625-632) and the centre's est v 615 lies on the plinth's front: either the route runs along the plinth's top in front of the doors (v ~555-565, about 0.9 m up) or the strip is nil; (3) u 25450-26600 the route is hidden behind the moored boats' mat roofs (est). The frame step set edge_n where structures_w had an estimate and structures_e or the wharf yard read the front (u 25350, 26300, 27650, 27850, 28050, 28250, and a turn at the lower shed's east end, 28000); the rest stand as structures_w drew them. The route through the dining terrace (22920-23500, reading (a)) is on the terrace floor, H 0 (support_ranges).
- **Section:** BOAT DECKS AND THE CREWS. Each deck's H is the water plus the painted rise from the waterline to the deck straight above (the river agents' two stroke points). The high-sided boats seen from above come out high: the cargo boat 1.77 m (190 px of side), the moored boat 2.0 m (208 px), the mat-cabin boat's main deck 1.31 m and its stern deck 3.17 m, grain boat 1's stern deck 1.96 m, 豪华客船's after roof 3.39 m (conf low). The crews then fall on their hulls in world D (worldcheck.mjs: every crewman lies between 0.3 m south of his boat's near waterline and 2.5 m north of its northmost recorded waterline point). Two roof crews were taken on the roof's level, not on the cargo they lie on: 豪华客船's reclining man (H 2.03; on the cargo's top he would be 3.74 and 2.6 m further south). The boats' painted lengths are short against their crews (grain boat 1 8.6 m, 豪华客船 16.2 m, 逆水行舟 19.8 m; 逆水行舟's mast 8.6 m), so the boat kit's real sizes are a recorded choice.
- **Section:** THE WATER LEVEL: copied from S4b (-0.7, a bound, conf low). S2's own checks agree and are no tighter: the landing steps at the terrace's west end give H >= -0.99 (river_w2), the bank at u 25250 >= -1.0 (river_m, weak), the east bank |H| <= 3.2 (river_e). No vertical face, quay or stair meets the water anywhere in S2; the warehouse's plank-faced plinth (u 25905-26240) is the nearest thing to a face, but its foot is hidden behind the moored boats.
- **Section:** SEAM NOTES FOR THE LEAD. S3 (u 20500): the cross-key check (20200-20800) finds no same-kind pair under 30 px, and the road, the water and both banks share their seam points exactly (0 px). The second pass (west) reads the small frame at u 20440-20530 (the merge notes' 'hut': uprights 10-20 px apart with a slanting rail, unlike S2's slat fence) as S3's by its middle (~20485), still recorded by nobody; and S3.veh.boat_moored_south_bank_19745's roof and planking run on to about u 20745 (S3 records its rear at 20640). S1 (u 29050): no same-kind pair under 30 px; the road shares its seam point (29050, 730) with S1.road.main (S1 marks it est); S2.grd.bank_lines_top_28900's edge_n and S1.grd.terraced_fields_29450 are the same lines read twice (2 px apart at the seam): one reading should go. S1 seeds from dx -51.6 at u 29050 and station +314.87 at (29050, 730).
- **Section:** TOOL QUIRK: plan.jpg labels the datum 'station 0' even when datum.station is set (S2: +179.21), as S4b's and S3's frames noted.

On the bank, as t2 read him: the small boat's near gunwale curves up from (22020, 1790) to its bow post (22127-22157, 1705-1780) south of his seat line (21890, 1673)-(22127, 1733), so he sits on the bank's edge above the boat, not inside it. DEPTH DIRECTION: the bed's lower edge (25535, 100) -> (25598, 72), slope dv/du -0.44 (it runs mostly east with a little depth; not a good receding edge). front/rear = ground under the crates' outer ends (est). Second pass, which reading: the barrow (kept). The two lattice crates are not hung level: the west crate (25530-25568, 75-98) stands lower and nearer, the east crate (25570-25600, 33-75) higher and further, one behind the other along a line that runs up to the east as a barrow's bed recedes here. No wheel shows: it would be under the crates.

THE BRIEF'S "SMALL CANOPIED BOAT" (24250-24500, v 500-600) is read as this boat's midship deckhouse: its two seated men sit at v ~600, on this boat's deck level, and no separate hull, stem or waterline shows under it. (3) A thin upright stick stands at the deckhouse roof's east end at (24537, 482-500) (t6 left it to river_m): a pole stood upright on the deck, or a stake on the bank behind the roof.

- `S2.tree.bare_south_bank_20705` (second bare tree on the south bank, behind the hall's west roof (uncertain)): Added at the merge: S3's t9 saw "a second bare tree whose twigs fan over 20650-20950, v 930-1390, its trunk hidden behind that roof (~20680, 1330 est)" (seen east of the seam, for S2) and its twigs mixing with the dark tree's east of 20650; river_w read "uprights at u 20610-20690" as forked branches of the dark tree; t1 recorded one tree, whose shoots it gives out to crown_e (20830, 800). Merge crops gen/map/S2/look/merge_tree_20705.jpg (zoom 1.4) and merge_tree_20705_foot.jpg (zoom 3): a separate set of stems converges downward at u ~20650-20715, v 1300-1455, 60-100 px east of the dark tree's knobbed head (u 20515-20610, v 1230-1380), to a narrow foot just above the hall roof's hip line (v ~1470 here); base est there (the true foot is behind the roof, on the bank between the water, bank top v ~1265, and the hall). READINGS: its own tree (taken, conf low), or the east shoots of S2.tree.dark_trunk_willow_20530 (t1, river_w); then retire this one. For the second pass: the tree count matters to the user. Second pass (west): keep as its own tree (conf low). On a greyscale contrast-stretched crop (g_fan.jpg, zoom 1.35, u 20430-20960, v 880-1500) and at zoom 2.2-3 (g_tree20705.jpg, z_frame20720.jpg), the stems split into two sets: (1) the long straight shoot at u ~20645 (v ~900-1340) and its neighbours bend west into the dark tree's knobbed head at v ~1340-1370 (its east edge u ~20615): they are S2.tree.dark_trunk_willow_20530's; (2) two or three stems at u 20700-20720 run down past the head's level, about 90 px east of its edge, converging to the hall roof's west hip line at about (20710, 1455-1480), where they stop as if behind the roof, and fan up and east toward u 20850-20950, v 950-1100. A drooping willow whip would spread downward, not converge, so set (2) reads as a separate stem whose foot is hidden behind the roof, as S3's t9 saw. Between the two sets, at (20720-20760, 1330-1400), a pale shape with folds that the restyle paints as a wooden frame with a wheel and a hanging cloth reads on the scan as a gap between the stems and the hip (no frame strokes). Points kept; its crown_w (20640, 940) may lie on the dark tree's shoot: the lead may move it to about (20690, 1000). FRAME AGENT (second pass accepted): crown_w moved from (20640, 940) to (20690, 1000), as the second pass offered: the long shoot at u ~20645 is S2.tree.dark_trunk_willow_20530's, so this tree's crown starts at its own stems' west edge.
- `S2.tree.dark_trunk_willow_20530` (great dark-trunked tree on the south bank at the bottom edge): SEAM: S3's brief gives t9 'the big bare tree with the dark trunk (base ~20330-20420 at the bottom edge, crown to ~20800)' and S2's brief gives t1 'the second dark willow trunk at ~20480-20600'. On the scan there is only ONE dark trunk here (zoom 0.8 view u 19900-20900): at the bottom edge it spans u ~20465-20600 (centre ~20530), with the S3 stall, its parasol and the hanging garments (u 20150-20470) west of it and no trunk at u 20330-20420. By its base it is S2's; S3's t9 may record it too: keep one. Trunk: u 20412-20610 at v 1350-1560 (a dark mass on its west side at u 20412-20460, v 1350-1500 is shadow or a second stem), u 20465-20600 at v 1600-1776; knobbed head u 20515-20610, v 1230-1380; pale limb from (20487, 1350) up to (20394, 1094); shoots' tips about v 700-760 over u 20310-20600, and out to u ~20830 at v 750-1000 (east tips mix with the whips of t2's great willow). The restyle paints figures climbing in its knobbed head (u ~20440-20600, v 1230-1460): not painted, only knobs and a pale knot at (20600-20620, 1316-1339). A pale patch between its east side and the south-bank roof's west eave (u 20600-20645, v 1630-1700) reads at a glance like a profile face, but at 45 x 60 px it is twice a figure's head here: the gap between trunk and eave brackets (the restyle draws a wall and brackets). Second pass (west): the pale patch under the hall's west eave is kept as S2.fig.figure_under_eave_20625 (low): at 45-50 px it matches the big-hat man's 40 px face at v 1712, so it is not too big for a face this near the bottom edge. The long straight shoot at u ~20645 (v ~900-1340) springs from this tree's knobbed head (it bends into the head's east edge at v ~1340-1370); the stems at u 20700-20720 do not (S2.tree.bare_south_bank_20705).

- `S2.road.main` (the wharf road along the north bank of the 汴河 (S2's route)): DATUM AND SEAMS: point 0 is S3's route point at exactly u 20500, (20500,258) est, copied from gen/map/S3/parts/structures_w.json with its edges (edge_n 215, edge_s 280). The last point is exactly u 29050: (29050,730), S1's datum: the open ground in front of the 望楼饭铺 shed's east end, just south of the rider's animal (hooves v ~690, t12); bare open ground runs on east at v ~650-860 in front of S1's stalls. EDGES: edge_n is where the open ground stops at building fronts, walls, fences, stalls and tables; edge_s is the river agents' bank_top (the level ground's edge nearest the water: river_w 20500-21850, river_m 23300-26000, river_e 26000-28900), copied at my stations; between 21850 and 23300 river_w2's part had not landed, so edge_s there is my est (t3: shore v ~470-500). No road edge is drawn anywhere. WEST (20500-21750): a strip only 25-65 px of v deep between the shop fronts and fences (v 100-141) and the moored boats (bank_top behind them, v 162-232). TRACKERS' BANK (21750-22300): the centre follows the trackers' towpath diagonal (their feet run (21867,235)->(22172,386)) and passes south of the split willow's feet ((22225,290), (22345,318), t3); river_w puts the bank_top at (21850,248) and the trackers on the top of the wash slope: river_w2's bank line decides edge_s here. HOUSES (22300-22920): between the screen/wall/gate/long house fronts (v 302-414) and the bank. TERRACE (22920-23500): no open ground: the dining terrace fills the bank (its front floor on posts over the slope, stilts to v 485-495). TWO READINGS: (a, taken) through the terrace's floor, from its west end by the long house's east doors and the head of the stone steps to its open east side by the pavilion, est v ~422-425, like S3's route through the tavern's open front (the lead's note for S3); the floor is about ground level at its back and on posts at its front, so the frame may give this range `terrace floor: S2.bld.river_terrace_23250` (a support_ranges entry) or ground; (b) behind the terrace, between it and the hall behind (hidden; the two roofs leave no room on the silk). The builder records the choice. EATERY AND YARD (23500-24600): in front of the panelled screen and the shelter's front posts (v 395-417), then across the wide unloading yard (open ground v ~180-490 between the top shops' tables, the cart and the bank), where the porters walk. WHERE THE HALLS FILL THE BANK (24600-26600): THE ROUTE TAKES THE NARROW BANK IN FRONT OF THEM, not the far street at the top. Evidence: (1) it is continuous with the unloading yard (the porters walk from the yard toward the boats at u 24425-24563, v 400-470, and the strip in front of the scaffolded building's wall foot (v ~500) opens from it, south of the stall under the building's sweeping west eave); (2) people stand on it (t7's two men, feet (25333,568) and (25367,587)) and a mooring post stands on it (t6's at (25207,548)) and the warehouse plinth's capped posts face it (u ~26018 and 26128); (3) at its east end it runs past the great willow onto the wide east bank where the jetty, the sheds' fronts, the sack porters and the 望楼饭铺 shed's customers are, and on to S1's stalls at v ~650-750, the datum S1 needs; (4) the far street (v 15-150, u ~24700-26900) is cut by the silk's top edge, shows only between and behind the halls' roofs, and runs into the top street at 王家纸马铺 and the 望火楼, which does not reach the ground at u 29050 (roofs and willow crowns there). WIDTH (a real-size problem for the frame and the builder): the level strip is only v ~500-520 in front of the scaffolded building (24700-25230; river_m: wash slope v 520-600 below it, where the mooring post stands), v ~555-613 in front of the halls (posts' feet v ~555), v ~600-641 in front of the warehouse and its plain wall: 10-60 px of v, 0.2-1.2 m at 51.25 px/m, plus the wash slope. At u 25450-26600 it is hidden behind the moored boats' mat roofs (est). It stays inside the painted area. The far street is a side street (structures_e). EAST BANK (26600-29050): north of the great willow's foot (t8/t9: (27068-27072, 893-905), on the slope), along the jetty's deck and the lower shed's front (river_e: the jetty's deck edge is the bank_top, at street level, u 27100-27400), across the porters' wharf just north of the sack piles (sacks v ~813-920) and along the long shed's front (posts' feet v ~640-690). Edges east of 24600 are my reading for structures_e to check. All edge and centre points hidden behind boats, buildings or willows are est. MERGE (2026-10-12): edge_s at the stations u 21950, 22100, 22285 and 22450 now lies on river_w2's bank_top (S2.grd.north_bank_22550: 311 est, 380, 441, 430), replacing this agent's estimates (300, 400, 440, 450), as its note asked. From u 22600 to 23300 river_w2's bank_top (418, 418, 407, 420, 432) runs north of this road's centre (410, 445, 448, 422, 425): the level strip in front of the long house is 5-15 px deep in river_w2's reading and the centre here runs on the wash slope, so those edges are unchanged and the frame step or the lead settles it. The steps at the terrace's west end are S2.str.landing_steps_22950 (river_w2's; structures_w's copy retired). FRAME: support_ranges u 22920-23500 on `terrace floor: S2.bld.river_terrace_23250` (structures_w's reading (a): the route runs through the terrace's floor); the frame gives that floor H 0 (its sill v 438-456 is level with the bank top), so the heights do not change; the range records the reading for the builder. FRAME: edge_n set where structures_w had an estimate and the front is read: u 25350: (25350, 470, est) -> (25350, 503), the open shop's floor line v 503-505 (S2.bld.open_shop_25340 sw (25210, 503); structures_e); u 26300: (26300, 610, est) -> (26300, 590), the white-wall house's wall foot (S2.bld.white_wall_house_26330 sw (26233, 592); structures_e); u 27650: (27650, 800, est) -> (27650, 830, est), the lower shed's front posts' feet v 828-840 (S2.bld.lower_shed_27780; structures_e, S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100); u 27850: (27850, 780, est) -> (27850, 830, est), the lower shed's front posts' feet v 828-840 (S2.bld.lower_shed_27780; structures_e, S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100); u 28050: (28050, 690, est) -> (28050, 560, est), the tiled shed's front, v 530-549 at its posts (S2.bld.tiled_shed_27985); the porters' yard is open from there to the bank top (S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100 edge_n 560); u 28250: (28250, 690, est) -> (28250, 560, est), the tiled shed's front, v 530-549 at its posts (S2.bld.tiled_shed_27985); the porters' yard is open from there to the bank top (S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100 edge_n 560); a point (28000, 828, est) added at the lower shed's east end, where the porters' yard opens north to the tiled shed (S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100 edge_n (27950, 828) -> (28000, 560)), so edge_n no longer cuts across the shed. Not changed: u 26100 (the warehouse plinth's foot, v 625-632, is the bank top itself, south of the centre's est v 615: the route runs on the plinth's front or the strip is nil; a question in the frame).
- `S2.water.bian_river_e` (the 汴河 at the grain wharf (汴水悍激)): No shore is inked. shore_n is read where the bank's ink-wash smudges end and the thin wavy current lines begin. West of the great willow it is hidden behind the mat-cabin boat (the bank in front of the warehouse shows only to v 640-650, where the boat's roof begins; est v 655-680; the true line could lie anywhere down to about v 850 behind the hull). East of the boat's stern the first current lines show at (26815, 890); the shore then runs down the wash-drawn bank below the willow and the jetty to v 1040-1110 (u 26960-27150; a dark wash tongue reaches v ~1150 at u 27100-27225). OTHER READING at the willow: t8/t9 put the willow's roots (27068, 905) at the water's edge, which would lift the shore there by about 150 px; I read the smudges at v 930-1100 below the roots as bank wash, not water (conf low). Behind the grain boats it is est at the boats' north gunwales (v 1110-1160); at u 28297-28357 it is t11's edge line under the bank porter (v 1203-1234); east of 28400 under willow whips and the massive willow's knobbed head (est). EAST END: the line turns south along the massive willow's west foot and meets the silk's bottom edge at about (28968, 1780, est), just east of the bottom boat's rudder tip (28955, 1768) and west of the willow's roots (u 28942-29255, v 1725-1787): no water is painted east of the willow (bare silk with bank wash at u 29150-29400, v 1450-1780), so the water does not reach the S1 seam at 29050. WEST END: (26000, 665, est), hidden behind the mat-cabin boat; river_m's line continues west. No shore_s: the silk's bottom edge (v ~1780 at u 26200-28700, measured) cuts the water all along the reach. 汴水悍激 (pin 7, u 26670): the current lines fill u 26000-27270 from v ~1000 (below the mat-cabin boat; from v ~890 east of its stern) to the silk's edge, densest and longest at v 1050-1500; eddy spirals at (26300, 1185) (a large one, rings over u 26120-26415, v 1155-1210), (26680, 1222), (27022, 1223) and (26908, 1495); thinner lines continue under and between the grain boats (u 27270-28100, v 1500-1780). The restyle's east half paints u 25500-26400 v 1000-1790 as dry grassy ground (its first draw, kept): it is water on the scan.
- `S2.water.bian_river_m` (the 汴河, middle reach (u 23300-26000)): shore_n is hidden everywhere (est, reading error about +-25 px): 23300-23700 behind 豪华客船 (river_w2) and the terrace stilts, whose feet reach the shore at v ~485-495 (t4); 23700-25150 behind the north side of the high-sided cargo boat (its bow deck at v ~575 and its deckhouse roof at v ~496-530 lie against the bank; the bank's dark wash runs v ~470-530 at 23600-24300 and v ~510-600 at 24700-25150); 25150-26000 below the bank's wash (v ~560-640) and the narrow bank strip in front of the halls (v ~555-640, t7), behind the moored boat's bow bitts, mat canopy and cabin roof (v ~600-680). No shore_s: east of u 22300 nothing is painted south of the water; the silk's bottom edge cuts it at v ~1776 all along 23300-26000. Ripple motifs (short paired curls) are drawn all over the open water, densest at v 1250-1650; nothing in this reach is drawn as a strong current (汴水悍激, pin 7, is further east, river_e). The restyle's east half paints u ~25500-26400, v 1000-1790 as dry grassy ground: it is water (the scan has the ripple lines). End points for the merge: west (23300, 492) joins river_w2, east (26000, 650) joins river_e. Merge: seam points set so the reaches join: west (23300, 496) est (the mean of river_w2's 500 and this unit's 492); east (26000, 665) est, river_e's (this unit had 650).
- `S2.water.bian_river_w2` (the 汴河 from u 21850 to 23300): No shoreline is inked. shore_n: where the north bank's dark wash and weed strokes stop and the ripples begin: v 455-520 at u 21950-22150 (a row of small shore ticks at u 22020-22200, v 495-520), bulging south to about v 560-590 at u 22250-22400 (the restyle's grassy bank there agrees), then hidden behind 豪华客船's bow post and cargo; at u 22955 the landing steps (S2.str.landing_steps_22950) reach the water at v ~488, and the terrace's stilts (structures_w) stand in the water to v ~485-500. shore_s: the south bank's mound meets the water at (21980, 1548) where the open boat is tied; south of that boat ripples run down to the flat bank's edge (21890,1673)->(22127,1733); east of the small boat's bow a low spit's upper edge runs (22150,1692) to (22300,1662) and down to the silk's bottom edge at (22680, 1790); east of 22680 the silk's edge cuts the water (nothing south of it is painted). SEAM POINTS: u 21850 shore_n (21850, 425, est), shore_s (21850, 1418, est: behind the mound's crest); u 23300 shore_n (23300, 500, est); shore_s ends at the silk's edge (22680, 1790). Water: open silk with ripples at u 22300-23450, v 920-1480 (nothing floats there). Merge: seam points set so the reaches join: west (21850, 345) est and (21850, 1432) est (river_w's; this unit had 425 and 1418); east (23300, 496) est, the mean of this unit's 500 and river_m's 492 (hidden under the terrace).
- `S2.water.bian_river_w` (the 汴河, west reach of the canal (u 20500-21850)): No shoreline is inked west of u ~21780; the lines are where the land or the boats stop and the ripples begin. SEAM POINTS: u 20500 shore_n (20500, 330, est) and shore_s (20500, 1235, est) are S3's river_e points (S3.water.bian_river_18300), copied; u 21850 shore_n (21850, 345) and shore_s (21850, 1432) for river_w2. shore_n: at 20500-20600 behind the north-bank cargo boat's mat roof, just south of the top willows' feet (bank top v ~272-290); at 20650-21100 it runs under the mooring rope's west end and behind the mat-shelter boat (its far side north of v ~240); at 21100-21700 behind the big cabin boat's far side, just south of the bank strip in front of the shops (figures' feet at v ~125-146, the strip's edge v ~160-175); from ~21780 it is the lower edge of the wash-drawn bank slope below the trackers' path (visible, v 330-345). shore_s: at 20500-20640 behind S3's boat moored along the south bank (S3's est v ~1220-1235); at 20640-20900 behind the branches of the dark-trunked tree (t1's S2.tree.dark_trunk_willow_20530) and the willow whips, north of the big tiled roof (top v ~1310-1340); at 21000-21300 between the covered boat's waterline (v ~1240-1250) and t1's people on the bank (feet v ~1370-1440), est +-40 px; at 21300-21420 at the great willow's foot (S2.tree.willow_great_21345, t2's, base est (21345, 1450)); from 21420 east it is the crest of the south-bank mound (S2.grd.mound_south_bank_21420), whose north foot is hidden right behind its silhouette. CURRENT: ripples are ticks and loops, densest in the open water at u 20500-20950, v 500-620 (round eddies at (20540, 590), (20640, 540), (20900, 560)) and along the north boats' waterline; strong swirls at (21880, 450) and (21250, 1145) and (21540, 1290); no bands of fierce current (汴水悍激 is river_e's, u ~26670). The restyle paints the water the same; it adds nothing on the water here except a tiled roof on the covered boat and a child beside the man in the water. Merge: seam points set so the reaches join: (20500, 310) est is S3's north shore (map/strokes/S3.json; this unit had 330); at u 21850 the north shore is (21850, 345) est on both sides (river_w2 had 425: on the merge crop gen/map/S2/look/merge_seam_21850.jpg the ripple lines start at v ~345-350) and the south shore is the mound's crest (21850, 1432).
- `S2.grd.bank_lines_top_28900` (two long lines at the top east of the 望火楼 (a raised bank or field edge)): Upper line (28750,150)->(29050,150)->(29200,145); lower line (28780,170)->(28820,188)->(28980,192)->(29073,192); a third stroke runs from (28757,167) down-right to ~(28857,242). They cross the S1 seam at u 29050 (v 150 and ~192; the lines here stop there) and run on to (29200,145) and (29073,192): S1's structures_w records "the bank lines and the diagonal track at the top (≈ 29200-29900, v 90-300)". The restyle turns them into terraced fields with a stone-edged path: invented. Two readings: a raised bank / field edge (grd), or a path's two edges (a road): the lead settles with S1's reading.
- `S2.grd.wharf_yard_28100` (open ground of the east wharf, where the grain porters work): edge_n: the sheds' front posts (the lower shed v ~830-840 at u 27570-27995, the tiled shed v 530-549, the 望楼饭铺 v 575-670). edge_s: copied from river_e's S2.grd.north_bank_26000 bank_top (u 27500-28900; its est points stay est); east of 28900 the bank runs under the massive willow (S1's by base). The porters' feet stand at v ~700-930 (t10, t11).
- `S2.grd.yard_pole_frame_27500` (open yard east of the 纸马铺, under the pole frame): Bounded by the 纸马铺's east end (u ~27290, v 200), the pole frame's feet (v 300-340), the gabled shed's door posts (u 27692, v 413-438) and the eating shed's roof top edge (v ~400-470). The restyle fills it with about twelve invented people; the scan has none (setup's check at zoom 3).
- `S2.grd.north_bank_26000` (north bank of the 汴河 at the east wharves): bank_top is where the level ground ends: the warehouse plinth's foot (v 628-632 at u 26000-26230; t7 reads the bank strip at v 555-640) and the plain wall's front (v ~640 at 26450); under the great willow's knob head (est, its lower edge at (26890, 676)); from u 27100 to 27400 the jetty's deck edge (the jetty stands at the top of the slope, its posts' feet on the slope); the lower shed's front and t10's mooring stake on the bank top (27902, 985); t11 reads the top at v 970-985 over u 28100-28400; est under the willows further east. The slope between bank_top and shore is narrow in the west (hidden behind the boat) and 250-300 px of v deep at u 26900-28400 (a long gentle slope, not a face). Bank top taken at street level (H 0). Things on the bank are the tiles' (t8-t12) and structures_e's; the wattle fence under the knob head (26850-26970, v 625-662) is left to structures_e. FRAME: its shore line stands on the support `water` (support_by), like the river's shores; its bank_top on the ground.
- `S2.grd.bank_n_23300` (north bank of the river, u 23300-26000): bank_top = where the flat ground ends and the wash slope begins. 23300-23700: the terrace (structures_w) and the eatery's front (bench feet v ~405, t4) stand above it; the dark wash under the terrace runs v ~460-500. 23700-24600: the porters walk at v 330-470 (t5); wash v ~480-530; a ladder from the cargo boat's deck leans up to the bank edge at (24045, 505). 24745-25235: the scaffolded building's wall foot at v ~500 (t6), a flat strip to v ~520, wash 520-600. 25150-25400: t6's mooring post at (25207, 548) stands on the slope; the two men on the bank (t7) at feet v 568 and 587; wash to v ~640. 25400-26000: the halls' posts reach v ~555; the narrow bank strip v ~555-640 (t7), hidden by the moored boat. The bank and the shore lines are estimates; the shore line is the same as shore_n of S2.water.bian_river_m. Merge: seam points set: west bank_top (23300, 452) est and shore (23300, 496) est (the means with river_w2); east bank_top (26000, 632) and shore (26000, 665) est, river_e's (the warehouse plinth's foot; this unit had 615 and 650). FRAME: its shore line stands on the support `water` (support_by), like the river's shores; its bank_top on the ground.
- `S2.grd.south_bank_spit_22400` (low spit at the bottom edge): The setup's first look put nothing south of the water east of u 22300; the scan shows this spit's wash to u 22680 (dark clumps at u 22400-22500, v 1740-1770). bank_top = shore: the spit is drawn as a low tongue with no face. Nobody stands on it. The mat-roofed boat's mooring rope reaches its top at about (22490, 1672). FRAME: its shore line stands on the support `water` (support_by), like the river's shores; its bank_top on the ground.

No vertical bank face is painted anywhere in the range. The restyle's six walkers and broad stone stair on this strip (u 22290-22950) are not painted (t3's check). Merge: seam points set: west bank_top (21850, 274) est and shore (21850, 345) est (this unit had 300 and 425); east bank_top (23300, 452) est and shore (23300, 496) est, the means with river_m's 470 and 492 (hidden under the terrace). - `S2.grd.south_bank_flat_21850` (south bank's flat edge by the open boat): Edge points (21550,1637), (21890,1673), (22127,1733) as t2 read them, checked here; ripples run down to the edge at u 22050-22130 (south of the open boat), so the edge is the shore there. West of u ~21990 the flat lies behind the mound and the timber frame and does not touch the water. - `S2.grd.mound_south_bank_21420` (south-bank mound: a long low earth bank at the water): bank_top = the crest's silhouette against the water (zoom 1.5-2 on the scan; t2's reading of the crest agrees within 5 px: (21600, 1402), (21700, 1402), (21800, 1403), (21870, 1440)). The shore is taken at the crest: the water shows right above it. The body of the mound is the wash at v 1430-1530 between the crest and the roofs (the roof's ridge from (21380, 1450) to (21900, 1650) is structures_w's). The dark band of low growth along the crest (u 21630-21810, v 1398-1420) is part of this mound; the shrub at its west end is t2's S2.tree.shrub_mound_21605. East of 21850 (river_w2's look range) the crest falls to the water at about (21960, 1530) beside the open boat. t2 left the mound to structures_w; recorded here as the south bank's own ground feature (the banks are the river agents'). If structures_w also records it, keep one. Height: crest about 30-40 px above where its foot would be, i.e. a low bank (est 0.5-1 m). Merge: river_w2 recorded the same mound as S2.grd.south_bank_mound_21700 (retired as a duplicate); its crest (21413, 1407), (21480, 1430), (21547, 1428), (21600, 1402), (21700, 1402), (21800, 1403), (21840, 1420), (21870, 1440), (21910, 1480), (21960, 1530), (21980, 1545) agrees with this one within 5 px and runs 20 px further east to (21980, 1545), where river_w2's S2.grd.south_bank_flat_21850 begins; the open boat's rope ends on its east face at (21905, 1490). structures_w's crest reading also agrees (it recorded none). The strip is very narrow: about 30-60 px of v between the shop fronts and the water (under 1-1.5 m at the frame's depth rate). Merge: seam points set: shore (20500, 310) est (S3's); at u 21850 bank_top (21850, 274) est, the mean of this unit's 248 and river_w2's 300 (on the merge crop the level ground under the lead tracker ends at v ~270-280 where the dark bank tufts begin), shore (21850, 345) est.
